### [1] [GæniceN: a minimal model to relax magnetization states self-consistently in general artificial spin ices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37889)
*H. Braun,E. Freeman,G. Alatteili,E. Iacocca*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出GaeniceN，一种基于三macrospin模型的迭代弛豫方法，用于自洽稳定人工自旋冰磁化态，并与Gaenice动力学计算衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工自旋冰具有可重构磁化态与GHz动力学，但其GHz研究受多模式及纳米磁体边缘磁化偏差影响；现有Gaenice需每个纳米磁体三个macrospin的最小模型，但缺乏自洽稳定的初始磁化态。

Method: 在Gaenice框架下实现GaeniceN：将Landau-Lifshitz方程的耗散项以迭代方式实现，高效收敛至弛豫态，自洽确定三macrospin磁化态。

Result: 以金字塔型ASI为示例，通过计算体系平均能量，正确预测了低幅外场下的相变；GaeniceN输出可直接作为Gaenice计算铁磁共振、能带结构等动力学的初始条件。

Conclusion: GaeniceN与Gaenice互为补充，可用于人工自旋冰的设计，服务于磁子学应用。

Abstract: Artificial spin ices are geometric arrangement of nanomagnets that exhibit reconfigurable magnetization states and GHz dynamics. Their investigation in the GHz regime is challenging because of the existence of multiple modes and the impact of magnetization deviations at the edges of the nanomagnets. A minimal model to capture such dynamics require three macrospins per nanomagnet, an approach that was implemented for generic artificial spin ices in the tool Gaenice. Here, we use the Gaenice framework to realize a relaxation method to stabilize magnetization states self-consistently, called GaeniceN. We implement the dissipative component of the Landau-Lifshitz equation in an iterative approach to efficiently converge to the relaxed state. As an example, we find our approach to correctly predict the transition between phases in a pyramid ASI subject to a low-magnitude external field by computing the system's average energy. Because they are rooted on the same framework, GaeniceN produces the initial condition needed for Gaenice to compute dynamics such as ferromagnetic resonance or band structures. Combined, these tools will be able to aid in the design of artificial spin ices with applications in magnonics.

</details>


### [2] [Anomalous Hall Conductivity as an Intrinsic Property of a Tight-Binding Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37939)
*E. S. Andriyakhina,G. Lemut,P. W. Brouwer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究弹道体系中反常直流霍尔响应何时可视为系统的内禀性质，证明在杂质散射弛豫存在下总霍尔电导 $\sigma_{xy}^{\rm H}$ 与轨道几何嵌入无关，但各分项仍依赖嵌入，并给出带项嵌入无关的对称性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹道体系中反常直流霍尔响应通常依赖微观细节（轨道几何嵌入方式、测量实现的具体方案），这使得"反常霍尔电导是否为材料内禀性质"这一基本问题缺乏明确判据。

Method: 将霍尔电导分解为内禀带项、侧跳项与反常分布项；分析各项在不同轨道嵌入（规范/几何表示）下的变换行为；引入杂质散射弛豫并考察总直流霍尔响应的嵌入依赖性；推导带项本身嵌入无关的对称性判据。

Result: 存在杂质散射弛豫时，总直流霍尔响应 $\sigma_{xy}^{\rm H}$ 与嵌入无关；但带项、侧跳项、反常分布项各自仍依赖嵌入。对称性判据可使带项嵌入无关，但该判据既不使带项等于总霍尔电导，也不使其成为 $\sigma_{xy}^{\rm H}$ 的可靠近似。

Conclusion: 嵌入依赖性可作为判别响应系数是否代表材料稳态内禀性质、抑或仍对测量协议敏感的诊断工具。

Abstract: In a ballistic system, the anomalous dc Hall response generically depends on microscopic details, including the geometric embedding of the orbitals and the precise implementation of the measurement. Here, we consider the question under what circumstances the anomalous Hall conductivity can be regarded as an intrinsic property of a system. We show that the full dc Hall response $σ_{xy}^{\rm H}$ becomes embedding-independent in the presence of relaxation by impurity scattering, although the individual (intrinsic) band and (extrinsic) side-jump and anomalous-distribution contributions to the Hall conductivity separately remain embedding-dependent. We further derive a symmetry criterion for when the band term itself becomes embedding-independent, but argue that this alone neither identifies it with the full Hall conductivity $σ_{xy}^{\rm H}$, nor makes it a reliable approximation for $σ_{xy}^{\rm H}$. More broadly, our results establish embedding dependence as a diagnostic of whether a response coefficient represents a stationary intrinsic property of a material or retains sensitivity to measurement protocol.

</details>


### [3] [Few-Electron Partitioning in the Fractional Quantum Hall Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37950)
*T. Jonckheere,J. Rech,B. Grémaud*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了分数量子霍尔点接触处N电子态的划分统计,在可解点g=1/2给出任意驱动下的非微扰电流与噪声公式,并证明电荷仅以e为单位转移、N=2,3,4时对称累积量展现出强反聚束与高阶关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受少电子划分实验[Shaju et al., Nature 642, 928 (2025)]启发,作者希望从理论上刻画分数量子霍尔点接触中少体(N电子)态的划分统计规律,以解释和预言相关实验可观测的量。

Method: 在可解点g=1/2处推导任意驱动下的非微扰电流与噪声公式;针对洛伦兹脉冲(Levitons)情形,将全计数统计化为N×N行列式,并计算N=2,3,4的对称累积量。

Result: 证明电荷仅以e为单位转移(而非分数电荷),即划分过程呈现整电子输运;对称累积量在N=2,3,4时显示出强反聚束(antibunching)与高阶关联,并可在(ν1,ν2)=(1,1/3)结中观测。

Conclusion: 少电子态的划分统计在可解点具有非微扰可解析结构,Levitons驱动下FCS归约为有限维行列式,给出整电荷转移与强反聚束及高阶关联的预言,为(1,1/3)结实验提供可检验信号。

Abstract: Motivated by few-electron partitioning experiments [Shaju et al., Nature 642, 928 (2025)], we study the partition statistics of $N$-electron states at a fractional quantum Hall point contact. At the solvable point $g=1/2$, we obtain nonperturbative current and noise formulas for arbitrary drives, while for Lorentzian pulses (Levitons) the full counting statistics reduces to an $N\times N$ determinant. We show that the charge is transferred solely in units of $e$, and that symmetrized cumulants for $N=2,3,4$ reveal strong antibunching and higher-order correlations, observable in a $(ν_1,ν_2)=(1,1/3)$ junction.

</details>


### [4] [Coherent control and high purity single-photon emission from telecom quantum dots on metamorphic InGaAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38060)
*Beatrice Costa,Bianca Scaparra,Hubert Riedl,Gregor Koblmüller,Eugenio Zallo,Jonathan J. Finley,Lukas Hanschke,Kai Müller*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用准共振与声子辅助激发系统比较，揭示嵌入弛豫InGaAs基体（位于变质缓冲层上）的InAs量子点的本征光学性质，实现O/C通信波段发射，获得21 μeV窄线宽、单光子纯度约10⁻²的g²(0)，并观测到三离子跃迁的相干控制与Rabi振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向光纤量子通信需要O、C波段的单光子源，而量子点发射波长延伸至通信波段时（如InAs/InGaAs变质结构）激发方案会引入额外效应、掩盖发射体本征性质，因此需要区分激发诱导效应与本征光学性质。

Method: 对生长于弛豫InGaAs基体/变质InGaAs层上的InAs量子点，系统对比准共振激发与声子辅助激发两种方案，进行光致发光及激发谱（PLE）、二阶自相关g²(0)测量，并考察三离子态激发态与相干控制。

Result: 获得窄至21 μeV的线宽，慢衰减通道消失，单光子纯度g²(0)达10⁻²量级；PLE揭示高阶激发态，实现三离子跃迁的相干控制，并在O、C两个波段均观测到类Rabi振荡。

Conclusion: 通过激发方案的系统比较可分离增强效应与本征特性，验证该类通信波段InAs量子点具备高质量单光子发射与相干操控能力，适用于量子技术应用。

Abstract: Semiconductor quantum dots emitting in the telecommunication O- and C-bands are promising candidates as single-photon sources for fiber-based quantum communication. In this work, we show how coherent quasi-resonant and phonon-assisted excitation help reveal the intrinsic optical properties of InAs quantum dots embedded in relaxed InGaAs matrices on top of metamorphic InGaAs layers for achieving emission in the telecom bands. By systematically comparing excitation schemes, we separate excitation-induced effects from intrinsic emitter properties. This allows us to observe linewidths as narrow as 21 $μ$eV, the disappearance of slow decay channels, and single-photon purity reaching second-order autocorrelation values in the order of $10^{-2}$. Photoluminescence excitation reveals higher excited states, enabling coherent control of trion transitions and the observation of Rabi-like oscillations in both telecom bands. These results confirm the promising potential of these quantum dots for applications in quantum technologies.

</details>


### [5] [Incoherent dark excitons extend Floquet-like physics across time and momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38156)
*Xing Zhu,Jacques Hawecker,Enrico Perfetto,Vivek Pareek,David R. Bacon,Harley Suchiang,Nanami Tomoda,Amrita Mitra,Takumi Fukuda,Sebastien Roux,Ouri Karni,Jack Featherstone,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Michael K. L. Man,Julien Madéo,Felipe H. da Jornada,Gianluca Stefanucci,Keshav M. Dani*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用时间分辨及角分辨光电子能谱在WSe$_2$/MoS$_2$异质结中证明，非相干的暗层间激子密集布居可杂化导带与价带的能量复制副本，产生类似Floquet现象，且寿命接近1纳秒；随激子密度增加能带由倒抛物线演化为墨西哥帽型，并通过扭转角实现动量偏移的杂化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Floquet现象依赖相干、时间周期的驱动（如强光场），其效应仅在驱动相干时间内存在，实际应用被限制在亚皮秒尺度；因此需要寻找突破平均场图像、具有长寿命的Floquet类电子态调控途径。

Method: 在WSe$_2$/MoS$_2$异质结中，采用时间分辨与角分辨光电子能谱（tr-ARPES），注入非相干、密集的暗层间激子布居，观测导带与价带能量复制副本的杂化；通过改变层间激子密度追踪能带色散演化，并通过调节扭转角研究暗激子的净动量效应。

Result: 暗层间激子的非相干布居实现了类似Floquet的能带杂化，效应持续近纳秒，远超亚皮秒的相干驱动极限；随激子密度增加，价带及其复制副本从倒抛物线色散演化为墨西哥帽型色散，符合理论预言的静态耦合（随激子密度线性增长）；改变扭转角后，具有净动量的暗激子产生与能带在能量和动量上同时偏移的复制副本并发生杂化。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了超越相干、零动量驱动的稳健长寿命电子态工程新途径，将Floquet类现象从亚皮秒相干驱动拓展至纳秒尺度的非相干激子驱动，并提供动量维度作为额外调控自由度。

Abstract: Floquet phenomena - where a coherent, time-periodic drive hybridizes the conduction band with energy-replicated copies of the valence band - have been demonstrated with intense optical fields and, more recently, other bosonic excitations. However, within mean-field approximations and the current understanding of Floquet phenomena, these effects survive only for the coherence time of the drive, limiting practical use and current demonstrations to the sub-picosecond regime. Here, consistent with recent theoretical predictions beyond the mean-field picture, using time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy in WSe$_2$/MoS$_2$ heterostructures, we show that incoherent, dense populations of dark interlayer excitons hybridize the conduction band with energy-replicated copies of the valence band, mimicking Floquet-like phenomena - now persisting for nearly a nanosecond. With increasing interlayer exciton density, the valence band and its replica evolve from an inverted-parabolic to a Mexican-hat dispersion, a signature of the predicted non-zero static coupling that hybridizes the bands and scales linearly with the exciton density. By tuning twist angle, we further show that dark excitons with net momentum create replicas that hybridize with bands offset not just in energy but also in momentum, providing another powerful degree of freedom beyond conventional Floquet engineering. These results establish a robust, long-lived route to engineering electronic states, beyond a coherent, zero-momentum drive.

</details>


### [6] [Charge Filling-Dependent Valley Polarization and its Dynamics Driven by Correlated Interlayer Exciton States in WSe2/WS2 Heterobilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38251)
*Zhiwei Li,Haonan Wang,Yubei Xiang,Wataru Idehara,Hailin Liu,Shinichiro Matano,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Kazunari Matsuda*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用时间分辨光谱与Monte Carlo模拟研究WSe2/WS2莫尔异质双层中层间激子谷弛豫随电荷填充因子的演化，发现整数填充（n=±1）处辐射复合增强、谷弛豫速率显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔体系中强关联电子与激子态已被广泛研究，但其对谷自由度及其动力学的影响尚属空白，而这对基础理解与谷电子学应用至关重要。

Method: 在WSe2/WS2莫尔异质双层中，结合时间分辨光学光谱与Monte Carlo模拟，测量层间激子（IXs）随电荷填充因子变化的谷弛豫动力学，并跨Mott转变进行分析。

Result: 整数填充因子n=-1处IXs的辐射衰减与复合速率增强；n=±1处谷弛豫速率显著下降。波函数交叠减小与声子散射过程受抑，抑制了非辐射复合与谷间散射。

Conclusion: 这些异常行为源于整数填充莫尔格点处引入载流子的局域化所导致的复合路径改变与激子相干性增强；表明激子与谷动力学可作为莫尔强关联态的灵敏探针，并为二维量子材料中激子相干与谷电子学性质调控提供途径。

Abstract: Moiré heterostructures of transition metal dichalcogenides provide an emerging platform for studying the strongly correlated electronic and excitonic states. The valley degree of freedom and its dynamics in the strongly correlated electronic and excitonic states in the moiré system are crucial for fundamental understanding and applications of valleytronics. Although the strongly correlated states in moiré systems have been extensively investigated, their impact on valley degrees of freedom and its dynamics remains unexplored. Here, we study the charge filling-dependent valley relaxation dynamics of interlayer excitons (IXs) in WSe2/WS2 moiré heterobilayer system. The evolution of moiré IXs dynamics across the Mott transition is elucidated by combining time-resolved optical spectroscopy with Monte Carlo simulation. Our results show that the radiative decay and recombination rate of IXs are enhanced at the integer filling factors n (=-1). Moreover, the valley relaxation rate of IXs at n=$\pm$1 is significantly decreased. The resulting reduction in wavefunction overlap and phonon scattering process suppresses the nonradiative recombination and intervalley scattering of IXs. These anomalous behaviors are attributed to the modification of IXs recombination pathways and enhance excitonic coherence due to the localization of introduced charge carriers at integer filling of moiré site. Our results explore exciton and valley dynamics as sensitive probes of strongly correlated states in moiré systems and highlight possible ways to control excitonic coherence and valleytronic properties in two-dimensional quantum materials.

</details>


### [7] [The Flexoelectric Twist: Persistence of Heavy Fermion Picture in Small-Angle Twisted Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38305)
*Qirong Yao,Ka Cheuk Fan,Tianyu Qiao,Hao Shi,Xi Dai,Berthold Jäck*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 小角扭转双层石墨烯（θ≈0.3–0.7°）中存在莫尔晶格重构与原子晶格膨胀，二者通过机电耦合产生局域重费米子f态，并与AB/BA位点的巡游c电子共存，验证了重费米子模型在小角区的持续有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一魔角附近TBG的平带为强关联与拓扑相研究提供了平台，但在更小扭角（θ<1°）下晶格弛豫显著改变莫尔势场；不同长度尺度的莫尔晶格重构如何通过机电耦合影响电子结构尚不明确。

Method: 对θ≈0.3–0.7°的小角TBG器件进行扫描隧道显微镜/扫描隧道谱（STM/STS）测量，获得空间分辨的dI/dV谱；并在唯象层面构建包含莫尔晶格重构与原子晶格膨胀（挠曲电效应）的模型进行解释。

Result: 在AA位点观测到显著dI/dV峰，谱关于电荷中性点不对称；证实莫尔尺度上的晶格重构及AA处的原子晶格膨胀。莫尔重构支持局域于AA位的紧致重费米子f态；原子晶格膨胀产生强挠曲电响应，显式破缺粒子-空穴对称性，将AA位f态下移，从而与AB/BA位的巡游c电子能量上共存。空间分辨谱定量呈现了这种共存。

Conclusion: 小角TBG中挠曲电性深刻影响低能电子结构，重费米子图像在θ<1°区域持续成立；小角TBG可作为探索相互作用近藤晶格问题的新型莫尔材料平台，且f占据数与c电子密度可分别通过扭角和静电背栅高度调控。

Abstract: Twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) near the first magic angle ($θ\approx1.05^{\circ}$) hosts flat bands that provide a fertile ground for exploring strongly correlated and topological phases. At smaller twist angles ($θ<1^{\circ}$), attice relaxation profoundly alters the moiré landscape. The extent to which moiré lattice reconstruction at different length scales affects the electronic structure through electro-mechanical coupling remains unresolved. Here, using scanning tunneling microscopy on small-angle TBG devices ($θ\approx0.3-0.7^{\circ}$), we observe a pronounced $dI/dV$ peak at the AA site of the moiré lattice on top of a spectrum that is asymmetric about the charge neutrality point, lattice reconstruction over moiré lattice length scales, as well as a dilation of the atomic lattice at the AA sites. We demonstrate at a phenomenological level that the inclusion of moiré lattice reconstruction supports compact localized heavy fermion $f-$ states localized at AA sites. The additional inclusion of atomic lattice dilation enerates a strong flexo-electric response that explicitly breaks particle-hole symmetry, pushing compact, localized $f$-states at the AA sites down in energy. Consequently, these heavy fermion states energetically coexist with itinerant $c$-electrons at the AB/BA sites. Our spatially resolved spectroscopic measurements quantitatively visualize this coexistence, confirming the persistence of the heavy fermion model in the small-angle regime $θ<1^{\circ}$. Our findings provide microscopic insights into how flexo-electricity affects the low-energy electronic structure of small-angle TBG. More broadly, our study establishes small-angle TBG as a new class of moiré materials to explore the interacting Kondo lattice problem with high tunability of the $f-$occupation and $c-$density through twist-angle and electrostatic back gates, respectively.

</details>


### [8] [Time evolution in the multi-component dynamical Keldysh models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38338)
*M. N. Kiselev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文为含长关联时间多分量随机高斯场的动力学量子模型构造了时间演化算符，覆盖SU(2)、U(1)、Z2三类对称性，在Keldysh（无限关联时间）极限下用Rice分布描述带非零均值的二分量随机场统计，并给出单粒子格林函数（对角与非对角）耦合线性微分方程组的精确解，进而以连分式法处理有限关联时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长关联时间涨落（如经典慢噪声、Keldysh型杂质散射）下单粒子格林函数的精确求解通常困难，需按对称性分类建立统一且可系统截断的时间演化描述。

Method: 构造多分量随机高斯场下的时间演化算符；按随机场分量数对应SU(2)（三分量）、U(1)（二分量）、Z2（单分量）对称类逐一分析；从无限关联时间Keldysh极限出发，对含非零均值的二分量场用Rice分布刻画其统计；导出并求解对角与非对角单粒子格林函数的耦合线性微分方程组；以连分式法系统纳入有限关联时间修正。

Result: Keldysh极限下含非零均值二分量随机场的统计由Rice分布给出，其单粒子格林函数（对角及非对角）由耦合线性微分方程组精确求解；有限关联时间效应通过连分式展开逐级纳入。

Conclusion: 该框架按对称类统一给出多分量高斯噪声下时间演化与格林函数的可控求解方案，Rice分布与连分式法构成从理想Keldysh极限到有限关联时间的自然衔接，便于进一步的数值或级数截断计算。

Abstract: We construct a time evolution operator for a dynamical quantum models with a multi-component random Gaussian field characterized by a long correlation time. We analyze three paradigmatic cases: non-Abelian $SU(2)$ symmetry class for the three-component Gaussian noise and Abelian symmetry classes $U(1)$ and $Z_2$ for the two- and one-component noises correspondingly. Abstract: Free fermions coupled to particle gain and loss relax to a Gaussian steady state, in which every natural orbital has its own occupation. We find the distribution of these occupations $\varrho_{\rm st}$ for a random Hamiltonian coupled to many random gain and loss channels. Each orbital is filled and emptied at its own rates, and the Hamiltonian, moving particles across the levels during their lifetime, averages the differences between the orbitals. We show that this averaging is incomplete: the spread of the occupations decreases only as the inverse square root of the number of channels per level. The spread suppresses the particle-number fluctuations below their binomial value.

Conclusion: 面向任务设计的知识库可使语言模型智能体具备开展可靠实验科学的能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents have begun to carry out laboratory measurements, but their scientific conclusions are useful only if they hold up under scrutiny. Demonstrating this requires controlled studies because models are stochastic and the context a model is given steers its conclusions. Here we introduce HERA, an autonomous agent designed to carry out cryogenic transport measurements of quantum devices. HERA draws on a knowledge base curated by lab members that holds measurement protocols, software tools, and literature references. We assessed HERA with a blinded trial against an expert on the stacking order of two multilayer graphene devices, an ablation of its knowledge base, and hundreds of offline replays in which models designed the trial's central measurement without running it. HERA's conclusions matched the expert's sealed conclusions on both tested devices. The ablation and the replay studies measured the impact of the knowledge base across different models, showing that with it every model designed better measurements and reasoned more soundly. To test how readily HERA extends to a new problem, we tasked it with finding and characterizing superconductivity in a different material system. This required a few hours of expert time to develop a new experimental protocol, after which HERA did both autonomously. These results show that, with a knowledge base designed for the task, language model agents are capable of sound experimental science.

</details>

This quantum three-wave mixing results from the BCD nonlinear Hall effect in noncentrosymmetric quantum materials taken to a dissipationless, quantum coherent few-photon regime. We show how nonlinear Hall materials can be integrated to superconducting microwave circuits to accomplish multiphoton vacuum Rabi splittings and dynamical stabilization of bosonic Schrödinger cat states. Such BCD circuit quantum electrodynamics are a concrete exemplar of a physical resource for quantum information processing enabled by nonlinear response in quantum materials.

Abstract: We investigate the thermally driven magnon spin current across a junction between a $d$-wave altermagnet (AM) and a ferromagnet (FM) or an antiferromagnet (AFM).

Abstract: Solid-state color centers embedded into mechanical resonators, where orbital states mediate interactions between spins and long-lived phonons, are a promising platform for hybrid quantum systems. The negatively charged silicon vacancy center in diamond (SiV-) is particularly favorable for strain-coupling to nanomechanical resonators because of its large spin-strain susceptibility and compatibility with device integration. Tensioned nanomechanical resonators offer an attractive route due to their ultra-long mechanical coherence times enabled by diluting defect-induced dissipation, though it is unclear if the same dilution effect undermines strain-mediated coupling to material defect centers. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a tensioned diamond nanobeam platform that enables enhanced strain coupling to SiV- orbital states. Through monitoring the optical response of the SiVs to the static and dynamical strain of the mechanical resonator, we observe that dynamical strain localizes near the clamps of these resonators under static strain. This allows us to enhance SiV-mechanical coupling and the mechanical coherence simultaneously, and provide a SiV-strain sensitivity with greatly improved accuracy over previous literature values. Abstract: Multilayer graphenes have emerged as a versatile avenue towards exploring novel electronic phases of matter. Recently, nanoscale multilayer graphene islands have been realized experimentally, where the island is less than 50 nm in diameter and distinct in layer configuration from the contact portion of the same graphene sheets, the latter of which act as leads for transport probes. Here, we theoretically investigate how the proximity between these islands and their chemically identical environment modifies the electronic structure. Within the tight-binding approximation, we find that the proximity effect is enhanced by the closely matched momenta between both parts, creating an unusually strong band renormalization which persists as the island size is increased, and only limited by the coherence length. An effective single-unit-cell Hamiltonian to capture these modifications is derived, expressed in terms of interlayer hopping parameters that scale with the relative island area. We predict a substantially altered low-energy density of states for 3- and 4-layer graphene islands, demonstrating the tunability of the electronic structure by spatial size.

Abstract: We investigate persistent charge and spin currents in a one-dimensional quantum ring threaded by an Aharonov-Bohm (AB) flux, considering generalized Aubry-André-Harper (AAH) modulation with staggered onsite energies and antiferromagnetic (AFM) ordering in both the noninteracting and interacting regimes, with the latter treated using a self-consistent Hartree-Fock mean field theory (MFT). At half filling, two distinct current-enhancement peaks emerge as the modulation strength increases, with quasiperiodic hopping modulation playing a crucial role in establishing the two-peak structure within a restricted parameter regime. Spin-resolved bond-current profiles reveal a switch in the dominant spin contribution between the two peaks, while the inverse participation ratio (IPR) and flux-dependent spin-resolved ground-state energies provide complementary insights into the spatial characteristics of the eigenstates and their spin-dependent flux sensitivity. At quarter filling, the spin current exhibits a single enhancement peak, in contrast to the two-peak response observed at half filling. The Hubbard interaction affects the two half-filled peaks differently: it suppresses the primary peak, whereas the secondary peak exhibits a nonmonotonic, re-entrant response, with the current recovering at finite interaction strength. The interacting results are further characterized through the Drude weight, and comparisons with exact diagonalization (ED) and density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations for a finite half-filled system support the reliability of the MFT description. Our findings elucidate how quasiperiodic modulation, AFM order, band filling, and electronic correlations collectively govern the magnitude and spin-resolved character of persistent currents, giving rise to multiple enhancement regimes and interaction-induced re-entrant behavior.

Abstract: Higher Chern insulators host multiple dissipationless chiral edge states, one per unit of Chern number. We study the collective plasmon modes of these edge states at a domain wall between Chern numbers $\mathcal{C}$ and $-\mathcal{C}$, using a semiclassical model with Coulomb interactions. The plasmon dispersion is linear at long wavelengths, with a velocity that increases linearly with $\mathcal{C}$. As in conventional two-dimensional metals, the edge plasmon generates an elliptically polarized electric field that decays into the bulk and drives electrons in circular orbits, producing a static orbital magnetization (inverse Faraday effect). For topological edge plasmons, the magnetization has a nontrivial spatial profile set by two competing length scales: the finite extent of the edge states and the plasmon wavelength. At fixed edge-charge amplitude, it grows strongly with $\mathcal{C}$. At the domain wall, it approaches a finite plateau at low frequency and decays as $1/ω^2$ at high frequency. Its temperature dependence is nonmonotonic, with a maximum at an intermediate temperature. These results show how the Chern number controls plasmon-induced magnetization, in contrast to conventional edge plasmons.

Abstract: This chapter explores the critical role of emerging non-volatile memory (eNVM) devices in advancing neuromorphic computing. Inspired by the human brain's remarkable ability to perform complex tasks like pattern recognition and adaptation, neuromorphic computing offers immense potential for implementing complex neural networks (NNs) in real hardware. Despite ongoing research into the brain's complexities, its fundamental operation relies on neurons (processing units communicating via electrochemical signals) and synapses (junctions between neurons). The modifiable strength of the synapse is key to learning and memory functionality, allowing the brain to adapt and rewire itself. For dense and low-power circuits in neuromorphic computing, these eNVM devices are ideal due to their synaptic-like behavior and non-volatile information storage. Properties such as conductance modulation of these devices emulate synaptic plasticity, which is crucial for learning, and their scalability makes them promising for large-scale neuromorphic architectures. This chapter will delve into the various types of eNVM devices such as resistive RAM (ReRAM), phase-change memory (PCM), spintronics-based memory devices, and their applications in neuromorphic computing, discussing their challenges and opportunities, and highlighting recent advances in their integration within neuromorphic systems.

</details>

Abstract: Twisting two stacked graphene layers superimposes a moiré superlattice that reshapes their electronic structure, yet how it shapes the collective optical response of finite, nanoscale bilayers remains largely unexplored. Using an atomistic tight-binding and self-consistent density-matrix approach, we compute the twist-dependent plasmonic response of twisted bilayer graphene nanoislands comprising thousands of atoms. The spectra are strongly tunable and split into two regimes at a critical angle where a single moiré wavelength spans the island; beyond it, an ordered ladder of resonances emerges whenever an integer number of moiré periods fits within the overlapping bilayer. In doped islands, the interlayer coupling also produces a moiré-enabled Born--Kuhn chiral plasmon: opposite transverse dipoles in the two layers form an electric quadrupole that hybridizes with the bright dipole, opening an anticrossing near the critical angle, with a handedness that reverses with the twist direction. Triangular islands confirm that the moiré lattice, not the flake boundary, governs both effects, establishing twist as a mechanical handle on chiral graphene plasmons.

Conclusion: 提供了条纹结构中带电C60分子的直接证据，并为设计具有定制界面电子性质的混合SAMs提供指导原则。

Abstract: Intermixed self-assembled monolayers (SAMs) provide a powerful approach for tuning inorganic-organic interfaces, but their local structure and energetics often remain elusive. Here, an intermixed stripe phase of fullerene $C_{60}$ and Zn(II)-5,10,15,20-tetrakis(4-aminophenyl)porphyrin (Zn-TAPP) on a Ag(111) surface is identified, establishing a direct link between molecular assembly and charge transfer. Scanning tunnelling spectroscopy (STS) and Kelvin probe force spectroscopy (KPFS) have been used to map electronic states and local contact potential differences (LCPD). Compared to close-packed $C_{60}$ islands, the stripe phase exhibits a larger interfacial dipole and increased occupation of previously unoccupied $C_{60}$ states, consistent with enhanced charge transfer from Ag(111) to $C_{60}$. Analysis indicates that larger $C_{60}$ spacing in the stripe geometry reduces Coulomb repulsion, stabilizing the intermixed phase and enhancing charge transfer with respect to close-packed domains. These results provide direct evidence of charged $C_{60}$ molecules in stripe structures and guidelines for engineering intermixed SAMs with tailored interfacial electronic properties.

Abstract: Dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) of periodic chains are determined by real Bloch momenta. We identify non-Bloch DQPTs, which occur only in open chains and are determined by complex momenta instead. They arise in a Hermitian chain coupled to a simple substrate, whose memory is treated exactly, and originate from the point-gap winding number $W$ of the projected propagator: for $W\neq0$, open boundaries change both the dynamical free energy and the critical times, even in the thermodynamic limit. We find that both follow from an analytic continuation to complex momenta, and show that $W$ can be measured by particle counting. This reveals a dynamical bulk-boundary correspondence, in which non-Hermitian topology emerges from Hermitian dynamics with memory.

Using the linear response theory, we analyze interplay of the induced on-dot pairing with the correlation effects, inspecting their influence on the conductance and thermopower. Our calculations predict negative values of the nonlocal conductance and reversal of the Seebeck coefficient when the quantum dot ground state is represented by the BCS-type singlet configuration. These phenomena are realized when the subgap charge transport is predominantly contributed by the crossed Andreev scattering. In contrast, on the doublet side, we obtain signatures for the Kondo effect manifested by the low-temperature enhancement of the charge conductance and clearly evident in the thermopower. Such temperature-dependent local and nonlocal transport properties can unambiguously identify the specific configuration of the correlated quantum dot and reveal the rich physics emerging in the doublet-to-singlet crossover region.

Abstract: Multiterminal Josephson junctions have recently attracted substantial attention. The ability to study Andreev bound states in a multi-phase parameter space opens up exciting avenues for generating topologically non-trivial phases in superconducting devices. A particularly interesting case is the trijunction based on a topological insulator (TI), which is predicted to host Majorana zero modes for suitable phase configurations (Fu and Kane, PRL 2008). Here we turn to the platform of the side-contacted TI trijunction and present experimental evidence for a multiterminal Josephson effect. We study the transport properties and find typical features of a two-dimensional critical current contour (CCC) as well as bias regimes in which only a single junction remains dissipationless. We pay particular attention to these features and study the effects of magnetic fields on single junctions within the trijunction, where, depending on the field direction, the supercurrents show Fraunhofer patterns, SQUID oscillations, or a monotonic decay. We find good agreement with semiclassical simulations for a single TI junction that take into account the top and bottom surface states. We observe pronounced non-convex shape deformations of the CCC under perpendicular magnetic fields that reflect the flux buildup in the loop formed by our trijunctions and provide a simple model sufficient to explain the observed effect. Our results provide the foundations for future studies of Majorana zero modes on this platform.

</details>

Abstract: We report finite-temperature scaling of voltage-driven quantum phase transitions in hybrid Josephson junction arrays. The critical resistivity and scaling exponent evolve continuously with electrostatic tuning. Where the three regimes converge, the critical resistivity approaches $h/4e^2$, consistent with the self-dual limit, while the scaling exponent approaches the quantum-percolation estimate. Tuning toward stronger insulating behavior raises the critical resistivity and shifts the scaling exponent toward the classical-percolation value, while both parameters decrease toward the metallic regime. These results connect scaling regimes previously reported in different two-dimensional superconducting systems through continuous electrostatic control.

Abstract: Rhombohedral multilayer graphene (RMG) has been demonstrated as a promising platform for investigating strongly correlated phases of matter. Here, we observe a coexistence of superconductivity and Chern insulators in a twisted rhombohedral bilayer-trilayer graphene moiré superlattice. Superconductivity, evidenced by a sharp transition with a critical temperature of ~180 mK, nonlinear I-V with a BKT temperature of 135 mK, and a signature of phase-sensitive interference patterns, emerges from a symmetry-breaking Fermi surface near v = -2 and D = 0, and it survives at a large in-plane magnetic field up to 2 T, strongly deviating from the Pauli limit. Moreover, we observe a strong modulation of the Ginzburg-Landau coherence length from 467 nm to 145 nm as the in-plane magnetic field increases from 0 to 2 T, whereas the critical current Ic barely changes. Unlike previous studies in RMG, we observe a competition between superconductivity and a correlated insulator at v = -2, monitored by displacement fields and twist angles. Note that we also observe integer Chern insulators with C = 1, 3, and -3 at v = 1/2, 1, and 3, respectively, for large D > 0 in the same superconducting device. The observations establish twisted rhombohedral graphene multilayers as a fascinating system to investigate unconventional superconductivity, high Chern number topology, and their interplay.

Abstract: Coupling quantum materials to electromagnetic cavities or resonators without external driving has gained prominence as a new control mechanism. Here we develop an equilibrium theory of a mesoscopic, disordered two-dimensional superconductor coupled to a subwavelength terahertz resonator, focusing on the interplay between superconducting phase fluctuations in the sample and electromagnetic fluctuations of the resonator. Modeling the resonator as a lumped-element circuit, we show that the coupling enhances, rather than suppresses, the superconducting transition temperature $T_c$. When the sample is smaller than the Pearl length, phase fluctuations near the Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) transition strongly amplify this enhancement. Crucially, the resistive quasiparticle response of the sample must be included in the analysis: at low frequencies the superfluid shunts the normal-fluid channel, reducing the total sample impedance. For representative parameters, a $1 \,μ\mathrm{m} \times 1 \,μ\mathrm{m}$, $8 $nm-thick disordered film, we find a relative $T_c$ enhancement of about $1\%$, an order of magnitude larger than the $0.1\% $ obtained when phase fluctuations are neglected. These results identify superconductors with soft phase fluctuations as promising platforms for equilibrium control with terahertz resonators.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有广义转移矩阵方法（Ghaemi-Dizicheh, PRB 107, 125155 (2023)）局限于无自旋或标量耦合情形，缺乏对自旋自由度与矩阵值（尤其非对易）规范耦合下非厄米谱与输运统一处理，也缺少区分Abelian与非Abelian非厄米输运的实验可观测判据。

Result: Abelian情形给出精确谱与闭式输运，揭示双极自旋依赖趋肤局域，并区分控制极点/零点/无反射的互易跳跃尺度与控制传输非对称、透明、隐形的定向放大因子；非Abelian情形出现自旋杂化激射与相干完美吸收信道、Zeeman可调激射阈值、方向依赖无反射输运及显著自旋转换。

Conclusion: 建立统一边界条件判据，涵盖激射、相干完美吸收、无反射、透明与隐形；自旋极化与自旋转换可作为区分Abelian与非Abelian非厄米输运的实验可及特征。

Abstract: We extend the generalized transfer-matrix method introduced in [H. Ghaemi-Dizicheh, Phys.\ Rev.\ B \textbf{107}, 125155 (2023)] to one-dimensional spinful non-Hermitian tight-binding lattices with matrix-valued Abelian and non-Abelian gauge couplings. We derive a minimal channel-resolved transfer matrix and identify its eigenvalues with the generalized Bloch factors. The same transfer matrix determines the periodic- and open-boundary spectra, generalized Brillouin zone, skin localization, and finite-sample scattering conditions. In the Abelian limit, the model separates into fixed spin sectors, yielding exact spectral results and closed finite-length transport expressions in terms of Chebyshev polynomials. These results reveal bipolar spin-dependent skin localization and distinguish the reciprocal hopping scale, which governs poles, zeros, and reflectionlessness, from the directed-amplification factor controlling transmission asymmetry, transparency, and invisibility. For non-Abelian gauge fields, noncommuting link matrices coherently mix the spin sectors, such that four transfer eigenmodes jointly determine the non-Bloch spectrum and transport eigenchannels. We show that this mixing produces spin-hybridized lasing and coherent-perfect-absorption channels, Zeeman-tunable lasing thresholds, and direction-dependent reflectionless transport accompanied by pronounced spin conversion. The resulting boundary-condition criteria describe lasing, coherent perfect absorption, reflectionlessness, transparency, and invisibility, while identifying spin polarization and conversion as experimentally accessible signatures that distinguish Abelian from non-Abelian non-Hermitian transport.

</details>

The radiative magnon-photon interaction is encoded in the resulting Poisson-bracket structure, while the cavity-induced modification of the magnetostatic field enters explicitly in the Hamiltonian. Coherent interference can suppress and restore reflection as the ramp slows. Abstract: Van Hove (vH) singularities can enhance superconductivity by generating a large pairing susceptibility. Yet, it remains challenging to experimentally establish whether pairing really occurs near these points. We show that the saddle-point dispersion near a vH point gives rise to a `light-cone' vH-selective behavior of Friedel oscillations, which is drastically different from the familiar concentric circles. Each vH point maps onto its own real-space light-cone sector, allowing STM around a single impurity to measure whether---and how strongly---that saddle-point quasiparticle sector is gapped. A superconducting gap near vH points suppresses the oscillations in the directions corresponding to peaks in the gap function angular dependence, a behavior that provides a signature of vH-related pairing at STM-accessible length scales. This STM protocol, targeting vH superconductivity, is capable of determining whether superconducting pairing emerges near the vH points or elsewhere on the Fermi surface.

Abstract: Tracking the resonance frequency of a mechanical oscillator is the basis of atomic force microscopy, mass sensing and thermometry. Nanomechanical thermal detectors exploit this principle to sense absorbed radiation, most effectively with tensile-stressed resonators, whose tension converts thermal expansion into a large frequency shift and dilutes dissipation. Approaching their fundamental sensitivity limit, however, has required free-space or fibre-coupled interferometers, hindering compact deployment and scaling to arrays. Here we remove this bottleneck by monolithically integrating a high-Q nanomechanical resonator with a silicon nitride photonic integrated circuit. A tensile-stressed silicon nitride trampoline with an impedance-matched platinum absorber is dispersively coupled to a photonic integrated microresonator beneath it. At room temperature and with microwatt-level optical power, the detector reaches a noise-equivalent power of 3.4 pW/$\sqrt{\rm Hz}$ for broadband thermal radiation (3-12 $μ$m), referred to incident power. Referred to absorbed power, this is within a factor of 1.5 of its fundamental thermal fluctuation limit. Using our detector in a state-of-the-art commercial Fourier-transform infrared spectrometer, we resolve the vibrational fingerprint of a polymer film across 2-20 $μ$m and reach the precision of the built-in pyroelectric detector with about an order of magnitude lower incident power. This architecture opens a route to multiplexed optomechanical focal-plane arrays.

Abstract: Electrical current bias offers a magnet-free route to nonreciprocal light propagation at the nanoscale. Both the random-phase approximation and the Boltzmann transport equation are used to characterize the nonlocal optical response of drift-biased graphene. We compare graphene on hBN with an image-polaritonic structure comprising graphene, a thin hBN spacer, and a gold substrate. Carrier drift produces pronounced directional shifts of the polariton dispersion, while coupling to hBN phonon polaritons strongly modifies the nonreciprocal response. In the image-polaritonic structure, the graphene polaritons become up to twice as confined while retaining a comparable Fizeau shift. The nonreciprocal polariton dispersion produces directional near-field emission and nonreciprocal dipole-dipole interactions, the latter quantified through the cross density of optical states, reaching a left-right dissymmetry of up to ~40%. Our results identify image polaritons in drift-biased graphene as a compact, electrically tunable platform for directional coupling between quantum emitters at the nanoscale.

Abstract: We introduce holographic microscopy (HM) as a compact, low-cost, label-free, non-specific alternative to fluorescence microscopy (FM) for quantifying bacterial physiological responses during drug screening. Unlike FM, which can suffer from photobleaching and may perturb live cells through phototoxicity, HM relies on refractive-index variations and maintains stable signal quality for long-term live-cell imaging. Abstract: Near-infrared (NIR) fluorescent molecules often suffer from low quantum yields, limiting their applications in sensing and imaging. High-resolution STEM-EELS mapping revealed that thin silica shells (4.4 nm for AuNRs and 3.1 nm for AuBPs) allow most of the near-field to extend beyond the dielectric layer. For AgNRs, a thicker shell of 7.0 nm was required to prevent oxidation while maintaining near-field accessibility. Electromagnetic simulations further evaluated the optimal shell thickness and the dependence of fluorescence enhancement on particle geometry, material, fluorophore internal quantum yield, and fluorophore-metal separation. Together, these results provide practical design guidelines for optimizing particle morphology and sub-10 nm silica shells in plasmonic nanostructures for fluorescence enhancement.

Abstract: In non-Markovian light-matter interactions, a spectrally structured bath can alter the evolution of a quantum emitter by modifying the vacuum fluctuations experienced by the emitter. The transient dynamics depend strongly on the spatial and spectral overlap between the emitter and the lattice modes: extended modes below the critical modulation generally produce rapid depopulation, whereas critical and localized modes give rise to coherent back-action and persistent Rabi-like oscillations, with particularly pronounced behavior at the critical point. Abstract: Optical spatiotemporal vortices carrying transverse photonic orbital angular momentum have become an intense research topic in the recent study of structured light fields. In this work, we theoretically and experimentally investigate partially temporally coherent spatiotemporal Laguerre-Gaussian (STLG) field with independently controllable radial and azimuthal indices. The spatiotemporal topological information, carried by the structured amplified spontaneous emission source, are well preserved in the presence of inherent instantaneous random temporal phase fluctuations. Under combined spatiotemporal astigmatism and group velocity dispersion, the partially temporally coherent STLG field can be converted into spatiotemporal Hermite-Gaussian (STHG) field. Both structures maintain their spatiotemporal characteristics after propagating through scattering media. Partially temporally coherent structured light fields may offer new insights into photonic topology and complex wave dynamics, with potential applications in high-fidelity optical communications and high-dimensional information processing in highly scattering environments.

Abstract: Sommerfeld's half-plane problem, describing diffraction of a monochromatic wave by a semi-infinite perfectly electric conducting (PEC) infinitesimally thin plate, is one of the cornerstones of classical electromagnetic scattering theory. Here we study its temporal analogue: a semi-infinite medium with the Drude dispersion, adjacent to air, whose plasma frequency is rapidly reduced to zero, thus turning it into air, while a monochromatic wave with frequency below the plasma frequency illuminates it under normal incidence. Unlike the PEC plate, the Drude medium supports evanescent penetration of the field prior to its temporal transition; the temporal interface here acts as the analogue of the sharp spatial boundary in the Sommerfeld problem, but now separates two time intervals rather than two spatial half-planes. We show that this transition forces the standing wave in air in front of the Drude medium and the evanescent fields inside it to propagate in both directions, while preserving their spatial field structure. In addition, we observe the excitation of additional electric and magnetic field waves generated by a "DC" current density associated with the time interface. Our findings offer insight into the electromagnetic wave behavior at temporal transitions in dispersive media and may find applications in time- and space-time metamaterials

Abstract: Topological photonic structures provide a powerful means of confining and directing light through interface states formed between photonic phases with distinct topological properties. Van der Waals (vdW) materials offer complementary opportunities for nanophotonics: their optical properties can be selected from a wide family of layered crystals, while their atomically smooth surfaces and lack of lattice-matching constraints enable complex photonic heterostructures to be assembled layer by layer. Power-dependent photoluminescence further reveals a pronounced nonlinear response of the confined Jackiw-Rebbi polariton, including a blueshift of up to 5 meV and correlated changes in its intensity scaling and linewidth. These results establish vdW photonic heterostructures as a versatile platform for engineering tunable and nonlinear polaritons localised at topological interfaces.

Abstract: In this paper we present a gauge-invariant formalism for describing the spin and orbital angular momenta of scalar and vector optical vortices. Abstract: Ordinary $0$-form symmetries $G$ can mix with $1$-form symmetries $Π_2$ to form a $2$-group $\mathcal{G}$. These relations identify the Postnikov class $[ρ]\in H^3(BG,Π_2)$ that characterizes the mixing. We develop a method to extract anomalies of these symmetries in one spatial dimension by restricting symmetry operations to an interval and comparing their composition near its endpoints.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Preparing complex quantum states of light for quantum information processing tasks often proceeds by converting from simpler states via experimentally accessible operations. We provide fundamental bounds on such probabilistic conversions, both exact and approximate. To do so, we establish a theory of weak, fully relative majorization that compares the disorder of phase-space functions. Our framework is versatile and can be tailored to different systems and sets of allowed operations. Focusing on Wigner functions in multimode photonic systems, we establish rigorous no-go results and tradeoff relations for exact and approximate conversions involving squeezed states, cat states, Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill states, and Fock states. In doing so, we derive novel bounds and recover known tight ones. Our results demonstrate that phase-space disorder imposes strong limitations on photonic state preparation.

</details>


### [65] [Magic in Grover's Algorithm and the Role of Oracle Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37772)
*Alessio Paviglianiti,Vincenzo Savona*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文刻画了 Grover 算法全过程产生的非稳定子性(magic),给出每次迭代末尾与编译无关的 magic 的解析预言(稳定子 Rényi 熵随规模保持 intensive),并表明已知解时可实现 magic 有界的有效分解,而无解信息的 agnostic 实现(如 3-SAT)会产生 volume-law 非稳定子性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover 算法是量子优势的典型范例,但以可高效模拟的稳定子态为基准,其在协议中累积的量子复杂度(非稳定子性/magic)如何增长、以及线路编译对 magic 开销的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 以稳定子 Rényi 熵为 magic 度量,计算每次 Grover 迭代结束时与编译无关的 magic,推导解析预言并分析其随系统规模的标度;随后考察将线路分解为基本门所引入的 magic 开销,对比"已知解"与"agnostic"两类实现。

Result: 解析预言表明迭代末端的稳定子 Rényi 熵随规模增长仍保持 intensive;已知解时存在有效分解,即便使用大量 Toffoli 门也能使 magic 有界;而 agnostic 实现(在 3-SAT 上验证)预期产生 volume-law 非稳定子性。

Conclusion: 研究澄清了 Grover 算法中 magic 的增长规律及"解感知"在线路编译中的作用,并表明 agnostic 情形下稳定子类方法无法有效模拟,而 solution-aware 编译可避免这一障碍。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm is a textbook example of quantum advantage, providing a quadratic query speedup over classical methods for unstructured search. In this Letter, we characterize the non-stabilizerness, or magic, developed throughout Grover's protocol, quantifying quantum complexity with respect to the efficiently simulable stabilizer states. First, we compute the compilation-independent magic at the end of each Grover iteration, deriving an analytic prediction for the stabilizer Rényi entropy and proving that it remains intensive as the system size grows. We then investigate the magic overhead due to decomposing the circuit into elementary gates. When the solution to the search problem is known, we show that an efficient decomposition exists that keeps magic bounded despite using an extensive number of Toffoli gates. In contrast, agnostic implementations are expected to generate volume-law non-stabilizerness, as we confirm for the 3-SAT problem, which rules out efficient simulation by stabilizer-based methods in practically relevant cases. Our findings clarify the growth of magic in Grover's algorithm and the role of solution-awareness in its circuit compilation.

</details>

Abstract: The characterization of possible transformations between quantum states and the quantification of entanglement are central for understanding quantum resources. Explicit results are, however, scarce since they require complicated optimizations over transformation protocols or state decompositions. We introduce a method to optimize functions of the entanglement spectrum of bipartite states over mixed-state decompositions with arbitrary accuracy. Our results are based on a convergent semidefinite programming hierarchy of optimizations over entanglement spectra using a natural polynomial basis and Schur-Weyl duality. Our approach can also be used to determine non-polynomial measures, such as the entanglement of formation, and to characterize the dimensionality of entanglement. Moreover, extracting the decomposition allows us to formulate criteria to determine state transformations under local operations and classical communication (LOCC). In particular, we provide a hierarchy of certificates of LOCC non-convertibility from pure to mixed states in any dimension and a heuristic approach for LOCC convertibility.

</details>

Conclusion: 新码在偏置噪声下具有优势，尤其Z缺失时可实现完美纠错，并给出了顶点阈值的严格下界。

Abstract: We study the error thresholds of a new topological quantum error-correcting code under the independent bit-flip and phase-flip noisy channel. By mapping to classical spin-glass models, we find that if Pauli X and Z errors are equally probable, the threshold of vertex syndromes is improved by 30% compared to that of the toric code, while the threshold of plaquette syndromes is lowered. Both thresholds increase when the Z component is less likely than the X component, reaching 0.5 if the Z component is absent. An analytical proof justifies this special case of perfect correctability, and a disordered version of Peierls's argument establishes a lower bound for the vertex-syndrome threshold.

Abstract: Magic states are a leading proposal for realizing fault-tolerant and universal quantum computation. At the present moment, much of the theory of magic states is not understood. Even simple questions such as what single qubit states have magic remain open. In Campbell and Browne's seminal paper on bound magic states, they prove the existence of states outside of the stabilizer octahedron which cannot be used to achieve universal computation. In this paper we expand upon their proof and determine a lower-bound on this region.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP态是容错量子计算架构的核心，但其量子资源性质仍不明；需要一套适配平移不变态的相空间量子化框架来刻画GKP态的准概率分布与零点结构。

Method: 在双离散圆柱相空间（环面作为构型空间）上建立协变积分量子化，导出Wigner分布与星形（恒星）表示，并分析理想GKP逻辑态、其叠加态、真空态及有限压缩GKP态在该相空间上的零点分布。

Result: 理想GKP逻辑态表现为双离散圆柱的相位本征态，其星形表示在全相空间无零点；其叠加态具有无穷多零点。真空虽为高斯态，但在该相空间上有零点；有限压缩GKP态的零点随峰宽与包络宽度移动，在宽度精细调节时进入该相空间。

Conclusion: 零点结构揭示了真空在GKP架构中的资源性作用：真空态的非零（及可调）零点特征正是使基于GKP的架构难以经典模拟的原因，为理解GKP量子资源的本质提供了相空间几何视角。

Abstract: Covariant integral quantization is developed on the double discrete cylinder phase space, with the torus as configuration space, and yields the Wigner distribution and the stellar representation. This phase space is particularly well suited to translationally invariant states, such as the Gottesman Kitaev Preskill (GKP) states. These states are central to fault-tolerant quantum computing architectures, yet their quantum resources are still under investigation. The stellar representations of ideal GKP logical states, which behave as phase eigenstates of the double discrete cylinder, have no zeros on this phase space, whereas those of their superpositions have infinitely many. The vacuum, although Gaussian, has zeros on this phase space, and the zeros of finitely squeezed GKP states move with the widths of the peaks and of the envelope, and reach this phase space for fine-tuned widths. These features echo the role of the vacuum as the resource that makes GKP-based architectures hard to simulate.

</details>


### [70] [Shor's algorithm compiled on cat qubits: 22 593 qubits for RSA-2048 in 3.14 days](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37839)
*Axel Pappalardo,Dahmun Goudarzi,Victor Hertz,Hugo Jacinto,Larissa Kroell,André Schrottenloher,Peter Shanahan,Élie Gouzien,Rémi de la Vieuville,Vivien Londe*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shor算法资源估计多为逻辑层或较粗略的物理层估计，缺少从数学描述到芯片物理比特分配与调度的端到端统一分析；本文旨在为猫量子比特架构提供完整、显式的资源估计与布局调度方案。

Result: 在1μs纠错周期、10μs反应时间、p_Z=5×10^-4且无比特翻转的假设下：RSA-2048约需23000个猫比特与3.14天；ECDLP-secp256k1至多30000个猫比特与1小时；FFDLP-512约6700个猫比特与21分钟；空间换时间时RSA-2048约611000个猫比特下1.25小时完成；加入距离3比特翻转码级联层后，比特翻转时间要求可放宽至约1小时，RSA-2048约需45000个猫比特。

Abstract: We present an end-to-end resource estimation of Shor's algorithm on superconducting cat qubits, from its mathematical description to the allocation and scheduling of physical qubits on a chip layout. We review modern versions of Shor's algorithm for RSA, ECDLP and FFDLP, and make their logical circuits and trade-offs explicit. We compile the dominant subroutines down to physical-qubit operations on two architectures: one based on repetition cat codes and one on LDPC cat codes. We design a parity-Cnot gate for cat qubits, extend a multi-target Cnot gate to the LDPC-cat architecture, and adapt an active injection procedure to cat qubits. Building on these primitives, we adapt to cat qubits the adder from Gidney's 2025 resource estimate for factoring RSA-2048. We combine our adder compilation and a coarser estimate for the phaseup with the factory designs and lookup compilation from the FeMoco resource estimation of Kroell et al. Together, these lead to chip layouts and schedules for factoring RSA-2048.
  Assuming a 1$μ$s error-correction cycle time, a 10$μ$s reaction time, circuit-level phase-flip noise with probability $p_Z = 5 \times 10^{-4}$ and no bit-flips, factoring RSA-2048, the configuration we compile in full, requires about 23 000 cat qubits and 3.14 days. Reusing the compiled operations, solving ECDLP on secp256k1 requires at most 30 000 cat qubits and 1 hour, and FFDLP-512, which we propose as an intermediate benchmark, about 6 700 cat qubits and 21 minutes. Trading space for time, RSA-2048 runs in 1.25 hours on about 611 000 cat qubits. The no-bit-flip requirement (a bit-flip time of about 100 years) can be relaxed to a bit-flip time of about one hour by adding a concatenation layer with a distance-3 bit-flip code, in which case RSA-2048 needs about 45 000 cat qubits.

</details>

Motivation: 现有和平方块编码及量子化学算法的资源估计多基于理想化或通用容错模型，缺乏针对cat qubit非对称噪声特性（X/Z访问代价不对称）与专用子程序的闭式编译成本分析，难以给出可信的物理量子比特数与运行时间。

Abstract: We provide a detailed resource estimate of the state-of-the-art sum-of-squares block encoding algorithm for electronic structure Hamiltonians of Low et al. on superconducting cat qubits. To achieve this, we present compilations with closed-form costs of the dominant subroutines such as skew-tree Qrom, a Swap network and a Phaseup for both a repetition-cat and an LDPC-cat architecture. Our compilation represents algorithmic qubits in the $X$ basis, taking advantage of the asymmetric cost of $X$ and $Z$ access that the concatenation of cat qubits with phase-flip classical code brings. Additionally, we provide a protocol for $\vert i \rangle$-state distillation and a two-level Toffoli-like $\vert \text{C}_X\text{C}_X X \rangle$ magic state distillation protocol, including three factory layouts. To minimize the cost of Clifford corrections induced by magic state injections we tailor injection gadgets to each subroutine. Combining our compilation work with the adder compilation from the companion work Pappalardo et al., we provide global layouts for the block encoding in both architectures which we use to obtain resource estimates for ground-state energy estimation for the benchmarks of FeMoco-54 and FeMoco-76. Assuming a cycle time of 1$μ$s, a reaction time of 10$\mathrmμ$s, a phase estimation precision of 1 mHa and circuit-level phase-flip noise with probability $p_Z = 5 \times 10^{-4}$ with negligible bit-flip error, FeMoco-54 requires fewer than $7\,500$ cat qubits with an expected runtime of under one day for a single phase estimation run using an LDPC-cat architecture. Relaxing the bit-flip requirement to $p_X = 10^{-11}$ with an additional layer of elevator codes raises this to an estimated $13\,000$ cat qubits.

### [72] [Exact verification of Givens rotation circuits for efficient quantum phase estimation of iron-sulfur clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37847)
*Lizhong Fu,Honghui Shang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种精确验证含寄存器角度Givens旋转电路的方法：剥离固定的Clifford基变换，在剩余电路中用整数条件追踪基态与相位，验证完整相干作用（含测量式uncompute与相位梯度态恢复）；据此简化加法器并融合两次加法为掩码加法，在FeMoco等基准上使相位估计Toffoli计数降低9.5%–14.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Under our interpretation of Low et al.'s angle grid and rotation convention, simplifying the adders lowers the rotation cost relative to the published accounting; fusing the two additions into one masked addition gives an additional saving. We verify both implementations exhaustively at every precision used in the published walks and prove the fused circuit correct for all widths. For benchmarks including FeMoco and other iron-sulfur clusters, replacing only the rotation term lowers total phase-estimation Toffoli counts by 9.5-14.3% under the published error budget, with the walk operator unchanged.

Abstract: We review a series of recent no-go theorems that extend the methodological and conceptual framework introduced by Bell's theorem to more sophisticated operational scenarios involving independent sources, quantum networks, joint measurements, and multiple observers. Many of these results were originally developed within quantum information science and are scattered across the literature, where their technical and application-driven success has partly obscured their broader foundational significance. We organize them around a common Bell-inspired methodology that extends beyond quantum nonlocality, and emphasize how increasingly rich operational structures change which physical assumptions can be tested.

Abstract: We formulate the standard quantum teleportation protocol within the framework of deformation quantization, employing the Stratonovich--Weyl (SW) correspondence and the associated star product on the coadjoint orbits of $SU(2)$. Each qubit is represented by a spherical phase space $S^2$ endowed with the Kirillov--Kostant--Souriau (KKS) symplectic structure. Within this setting, we explicitly construct the phase space representations of the entangled Bell resource, the controlled-NOT and Hadamard operators, the projective measurements, and the conditional Pauli corrections. We demonstrate how the quantum information initially encoded in the Wigner function for the input qubit is redistributed through bipartite and tripartite phase space correlations. Upon consideration of a conditional unitary transformation, the measurement outcome produces a Wigner function on the receiver's coadjoint orbit that is related to the input by an outcome-dependent rotation, which can subsequently be inverted through the implementation of an appropriate Pauli correction. The covariance of the SW correspondence provides a geometric interpretation of this process, in which quantum information is redistributed and reconstructed without transferring the underlying symplectic geometry. Our formulation can also be extended to study quantum teleportation in more general phase space geometries equipped with suitable star products.

Abstract: With D. Aerts, we showed in 2014, within the extended Bloch representation of quantum mechanics, that the Born rule can be understood as resulting from a universal average over all the possible ways in which the measurement-interactions can be selected. The cellular construction used there was since questioned, in the context of Bertrand's paradox, by N. Shackel, who observed that the result depends on the discretization adopted, that the sequence of averages is constant, and that the sense in which the average is taken over "all" densities remains unclear. Taking these criticisms as a stimulus, we re-examine the notion. The key is to recognize that a universal measurement is a random way of selecting a measurement which does not privilege any portion of the structure describing the available interactions, a property we call indifference. We prove that every indifferent universal measurement, however constructed, produces exactly the Born probabilities: the universal average is unique, and the cellular construction of 2014 is one among infinitely many ways of obtaining it, exact at every finite resolution. Non-trivial indifferent universal measurements exist, but must involve deterministic measurements: Dirichlet processes are indifferent universal measurements reaching every way of selecting an interaction, whereas an indifferent universal measurement free of deterministic components necessarily selects the uniform density, so that the Born rule holds not only on average but with certainty. Deviations from the quantum statistics in sequences of measurements are governed by a single parameter, in principle accessible to experiment. The only geometric inputs are the Euclidean length of the membrane and the orthogonal landing of the point-particle on it; the latter is where the content of Gleason's theorem lies. Generalizations to N outcomes and to Bertrand's paradox are discussed.

</details>

Abstract: A unified Fourier framework for quantum weight enumerators of prime-power-dimensional qudits is developed in this paper. We introduce the notions of Weyl power spectrum and Weyl-conjugation autocorrelation for the Weyl-operator quantities nderlying the Shor--Laflamme weight enumerators. Representing these quantities as formal vectors, we show that they form a symplectic Fourier transform pair, from which the quantum MacWilliams identity follows by applying the Hamming-weight functional. This framework extends to a family of twisted Fourier transforms, including the Rains shadow transform. We show that identical-copy Bell sampling corresponds to a twisted autocorrelation related to the shadow enumerator through Weyl translation and Hamming projection. We further prove that all twisted Fourier transforms are Weyl translations of either the ordinary MacWilliams transform or the Rains shadow transform. This two-class reduction implies that the twists yield no additional independent coefficientwise positivity constraints under compatible projections. Nevertheless, the framework leads to refined MacWilliams and shadow identities that retain more Fourier information than ordinary Hamming weight enumerators. We employ these refined identities in linear programming, together with simplex mapping and shadow-coset counting, to exclude surviving candidate weight distributions. These techniques yield tighter bounds for binary and qutrit quantum codes, including nonexistence results for specific code parameters.

Abstract: The Pusey--Barrett--Rudolph (PBR) theorem is commonly taken to establish that a pure quantum state represents a physical property of the system to which it is assigned, rather than merely encoding knowledge. In this work, I distinguish between state-first and dynamics-first physical theories, and argue that this reading presupposes the former. These two types of theories support different approaches to causal explanation, and one of PBR's stated assumptions depends on the state-first approach. Using a recent interpretation of quantum theory as a concrete example, I show how a dynamics-first theory can explain the PBR correlations while maintaining a broadly epistemic reading of quantum states. I conclude that the PBR theorem supports a dilemma: either the quantum state is part of reality, or reality is not fundamentally built out of states.

</details>

As a result, the preparation error decreases cubically instead of quadratically, and the asymptotic raw-copy overhead is reduced. We also construct a preliminary sequence in which a processed block is paired with a fresh copy from the same noisy source. At a common source fidelity, it reduces the expected cost of preparing an input for the recurrence by more than seven orders of magnitude relative to the balanced construction studied here. The additional layer leaves the leading operation-error coefficient unchanged, although a nonzero error floor remains.

Result: 独立评估的偏差与估计代价能同时预测最优上下文半径与最优误差；良好采样区估计代价随半径指数增长；与能隙区近似指数偏差衰减竞争预测误差随N的幂律衰减，临界处代数偏差衰减预测渐近随ln N的反幂律衰减；体点结果与对应有限样本预测一致。

Conclusion: 该框架把量子测量分布的结构与有限数据所能支撑的模型容量定量联系起来，为生成学习中容量选择的标度律提供了可验证的理论依据。

Abstract: Generative learning of a quantum measurement distribution from finite data requires choosing a model capacity that balances representation accuracy against estimation cost. We develop a bias-variance framework that decomposes the expected population Kullback-Leibler error at model capacity $s$ and sample size $N$ as $\mathcal E(s,N)=B(s)+V(s,N)$, with representation bias $B$ and a variance term $V$ capturing the mean estimation error. In the well-sampled regime, $V(s,N)\simeq[NC(s)]^{-1}$, and exponential or algebraic laws for $B$ and $C$ yield four scaling templates for the optimal error, from inverse powers of $N$ to inverse powers of $\ln N$. For local autoregressive models, the context radius sets the model capacity, and $B$ is the summed conditional mutual information lost by truncating the context. For a smoothed histogram learner, we derive an exact finite-sample estimation cost that accounts for rarely observed contexts and recovers the parameter-count law in the well-sampled regime. For transverse-field Ising Born distributions, the independently evaluated bias and estimation cost predict both the optimal context radius and the optimal error. In the well-sampled regime, the estimation cost grows exponentially with context radius. Its competition with approximately exponential bias decay in the gapped regimes predicts power-law error decay in $N$, while algebraic bias decay at criticality predicts asymptotic inverse-power decay in $\ln N$. Bulk-site results are consistent with the corresponding finite-sample predictions. These results connect the structure of quantum measurement distributions to the model capacity justified by finite data.

</details>


### [80] [Continuous variable quantum key distribution through a fading channel with minimal information leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38048)
*Josephine Dias,Timothy C. Ralph*

TL;DR: 分析一种无信息泄露型连续变量量子密钥分发协议在衰落信道（自由空间链路模型）下的性能，发现其表现稳健并优于同类协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离CV-QKD的核心挑战是光学损耗，其限制了密钥率与可达传输距离；已有工作提出了在纯损耗信道下消除、在噪声信道下最小化窃听者信息泄露的方案，但尚未考察自由空间衰落信道下的适用性。

Method: 将连接收发双方的量子信道建模为衰落信道（用于描述自由空间链路），在此条件下对原有协议进行安全性/性能分析，并与可比协议进行对比。

Result: 该协议在衰落信道上仍保持较强性能，且性能优于同类（comparable）协议。

Conclusion: 所分析的CV-QKD协议适用于衰落信道场景，具备推广至自由空间量子通信的潜力。

Abstract: Continuous variable quantum key distribution enables two parties to extract provably secure keys for cryptography by exploiting aspects of quantum mechanics. A central challenge in achieving QKD over long distances is overcoming optical loss, which limits the secret key rates and achievable transmission distances. Recent work has illustrated how to perform continuous variable quantum key distribution while eliminating information leakage to an eavesdropper in a pure-loss channel and minimizing information leakage in a noisy channel. In this work, we analyse this protocol under conditions where the quantum channel linking the sender and receiver is a fading channel, which can be used to model free-space links. We find that the protocol maintains strong performance over fading channels and outperforms comparable protocols.

</details>


### [81] [Perspective on AI and quantum open-source software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38049)
*Changhao Li,Bradley A. Chase,Veena Vijayakumar,Mathys Rennela,Alessandro Cosentino,Naixu Guo,Sebastian Hassinger,Farrokh Labib,Seun Omonije,Patrick Rebentrost,Vincent Russo,Nathan Shammah,William J. Zeng,Ben Castanon*

TL;DR: 该观点文章将"可验证性"定位为AI辅助量子开源软件开发与研究的核心要求，基于110个仓库、5届量子开源黑客松记录及1600余名社区调查参与者的分析，提出以可复现证据支撑贡献的验证实践与贡献标准，并提供开源配套仓库以推动落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开源软件是量子科学的关键基础设施，AI正在重塑其能力与开发方式；但大规模量子软件验证仍很困难，AI生成的贡献还会加重维护者的审查负担。作者希望厘清自动化验证的局限与维护者审查负担之间的联系，从而为可靠的AI辅助量子软件开发确立规范。

Method: 采用观点（Perspective）形式，结合多源实证材料：公开仓库分析、作者组织的五届年度量子开源黑客松所覆盖的110个仓库的记录，以及1600余名参与者的社区问卷调查；据此分析验证能力不足与维护负担之间的关系，并提出验证实践与贡献标准（要求贡献者以可复现证据支持其改动），同时维护一个开源配套仓库以支持复现与项目采纳。

Result: 分析表明，自动化验证的局限与量子软件维护者的审查负担相互关联；据此提出的贡献标准要求提交改动时附带可复现证据，供维护者审查。文章还讨论了量子开源软件如何为AI驱动的研究提供基础设施，使社区能够独立验证其结果并在此基础上继续构建。

Conclusion: 通过强调可复现验证，量子开源软件社区可将AI进展转化为可靠的量子研究成果；配套开源仓库有助于复现并推动各项目采纳与完善这些提议。

Abstract: Quantum open-source software provides essential infrastructure for quantum science, and artificial intelligence (AI) is reshaping its capabilities and transforming its development. However, verifying quantum software at scale remains difficult, and AI-generated contributions can increase the workload of maintainers. In this Perspective, we examine verification as a central requirement for reliable AI-assisted quantum software development and research. We analyze public repositories, records covering 110 repositories from five annual quantum open-source hackathons that we organize, and responses from more than 1600 participants in a community survey. Our analysis connects limitations in automated verification with the review burden on quantum software maintainers. We propose verification practices and contribution standards that require contributors to support their changes with reproducible evidence for maintainer review. We also examine how quantum open-source software can provide infrastructure for AI-driven research whose results the community can independently verify and build upon. We maintain an open-source companion repository to support reproducibility and help projects adopt and refine these proposals. Together, these contributions help the quantum open-source software community translate advances in AI into reliable quantum research through reproducible verification.

</details>


### [82] [Exact Positive-P Simulation of Open Quantum Spin Systems with Generalized Density Matrix Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38059)

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于局域广义密度矩阵(GDM)的相位空间表示方法，实现自旋-1/2开放量子系统的精确正-P模拟，并可无缝耦合玻色子正-P方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色子正-P表示可高效精确模拟多体开放量子系统，但前提是耗散足够强以稳定随机轨迹。自旋体系对量子技术至关重要，而以往基于SU(2)相干态的自旋正-P表示即使数值稳定也可能出现系统性偏差，缺乏可靠的可扩展精确方法。

Method: 不再使用SU(2)相干态，而是将自旋-1/2自由度映射为局域广义密度矩阵(GDM)的参数，构建新的相位空间表示；并与玻色子正-P表示结合以处理自旋-玻色耦合体系。以Jaynes-Cummings模型绘制自旋与玻色耗散下的模拟稳定性相图，并应用于Jaynes-Cummings-Hubbard准Lieb晶格。

Result: 在所有稳定区域内未观察到与精确解的偏差，恢复了正-P性质：稳定解在随机采样噪声范围内精确。该方法可高度可扩展地处理耦合自旋-玻色系统；在JC-Hubbard准Lieb晶格中展示了自旋耗散与驱动可破坏或恢复激发不可压缩性。

Conclusion: GDM相位空间表示为自旋-1/2开放量子系统提供了稳定即可信的可扩展精确模拟框架，并自然推广到自旋-玻色耦合多体问题，为量子技术相关的自旋体系提供实用数值工具。

Abstract: The positive-P representation for bosons allows scalable exact simulations of many-body open quantum systems, provided dissipation is strong enough to stabilise stochastic trajectories. Designing analogous methods for spin systems, relevant to quantum technologies, remains challenging, as previous spin positive-P formulations can exhibit systematic errors even when simulations are numerically stable. Here, we develop a phase-space representation that maps spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ degrees of freedom onto parameters of a local generalized density matrix (GDM), rather than SU(2) coherent states. In all stable regimes, we observe no deviations from exact solutions, restoring the positive-P property: stable solutions are exact up to stochastic sampling noise. The GDM representation combines seamlessly with bosonic positive-P, enabling highly scalable treatment of coupled spin-boson systems. We map simulation stability versus spin and boson dissipation in the Jaynes-Cummings model and apply the method to a Jaynes-Cummings-Hubbard quasi-Lieb lattice, showing how spin dissipation and driving can destroy or restore excitation incompressibility.

</details>
