Abstract: A coherent ensemble of polarized helium-3 nuclei and free neutrons are coupled, forming a neutron-nucleus hybrid system. The nuclei are prepared in a spin superposition, while a magnetic field induces Zeeman splitting. The resulting nuclear energy phase is coherently transferred to the neutron, producing an intensity beat at the nuclear Zeeman frequency. Finally, the incident neutron is prepared in a spin-energy superposition state. The nuclear and neutron phases then heterodyne coherently, producing a signal at the sum or difference frequency depending on their relative helicity.

</details>


### [84] [Experimental Observation of Error-Resilience Phase Transition on Superconducting Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38148)
*Emanuel Dallas,Mario Morford-Oberst,Eric Weine*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在IBM超导量子设备上首次实验观测噪声随机编解码电路中的错误保护—脆弱相变，用保真度、Rényi-2纠缠熵与稳定子Rényi-2熵三个序参量及随机测量、零噪声外推和M3 SPAM缓解，给出α_c≈1.01，低于理论值约1.14。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机幺正可把量子信息扰动至高度非局域自由度从而抵抗局域噪声，但Turkeshi与Sierant指出局域噪声足够强时该保护会失效，并预言存在保护相与脆弱相之间的相变（临界相干噪声角α_c≈1.14）。此前缺乏实验验证，本文旨在首次在真实硬件上观测该相变并检验理论预言的临界点。

Method: 在IBM ibm_pittsburgh Heron r3设备上运行含噪声的随机编码-解码电路，系统规模至N=26量子比特；以保真度、Rényi-2纠缠熵和稳定子Rényi-2熵作为序参量，后两者通过随机测量协议构造估计量；全部序参量采用零噪声外推与M3 SPAM误差缓解；再用高斯过程回归对三个序参量的有限尺寸标度塌缩进行联合建模以提取临界角，并对比无噪声模拟数据与未缓解原始数据的分析结果。

Result: 实验联合拟合给出α_c≈1.01，显著低于理论值≈1.14；将无噪声模拟数据输入同一模型得到α_c≈1.05，说明实验与理论的偏差主要源于编码电路深度不足、未达到真正Haar随机；同一模型无法在未经缓解的原始实验数据中可靠识别临界行为。

Conclusion: 本文首次在超导量子处理器上实验观测到噪声随机编解码电路中的保护—脆弱相变；临界角偏离理论值可归因于有限深度编码电路的非Haar性；误差缓解技术（零噪声外推与M3 SPAM）对在真实硬件上探测该相变至关重要。

Abstract: Random unitaries scramble quantum information into highly nonlocal degrees of freedom, thereby providing protection from local noise. However, as demonstrated by Turkeshi and Sierant (Phys. Rev. Lett. 132, 140401, 2024 and arXiv:2603.00235), this protection may fail once local noise grows sufficiently strong. These works describe a phase transition in a noisy random encoding-decoding circuit model between an error-protecting phase, in which encoded information is recoverable, and an error-vulnerable phase, in which encoded information is lost, with the transition occurring at a critical coherent noise angle $α_c \approx 1.14$. Here, we present the first experimental observation of this transition, conducted on IBM's ibm_pittsburgh Heron r3 device for system sizes of up to $N = 26$ qubits. We detect this transition via three order parameters: fidelity, Rényi-2 entanglement entropy, and stabilizer Rényi-2 entropy. For the latter two, we employ randomized measurement protocols to construct estimators, and for all three, we rely on zero-noise extrapolation and M3 SPAM error mitigation. We then jointly model the finite-size scaling collapse across all three order parameters with a Gaussian process regression, yielding a critical angle $α_c \approx 1.01$, well below the theoretical value. Inputting noiseless simulated data into the same model yields $α_c \approx 1.05$, implying that the discrepancy between experiment and theory is due to our encoding circuits, which are not deep enough to be truly Haar-random. Finally, we observe that the same model cannot adequately identify critical behavior in unmitigated, raw experimental data. This indicates that error mitigation techniques are crucial for detecting the transition on hardware.

</details>


### [85] [Quantum advantage by designing beneficial dissipation from lossless dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38183)
*Zeng-Zhao Li,Xiao Xue*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 被动损耗在经典群体动力学中服从"有限强度单调性"，无法提升无条件目标；但相干量子动力学可通过相消干涉违反该单调性。作者导出基于无损耗动力学的响应算符判据，其谱决定弱被动损耗是否有益、主特征向量给出最优环境模式，并在振动辅助传能中实现8.73%的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直觉上，被动损耗只是从系统中移除概率，似乎不可能改善无条件目标（如总传能效率）。在经典群体动力学、仅"汇"式移除下，这一直觉可被严格化为有限强度单调性原理，即便对剩余控制量做优化也成立。作者要追问：量子相干动力学是否能突破这一经典限制，以及如何在无需有限损耗优化的前提下预先判断并设计有益的被动环境。

Method: 1) 以经典sink-only移除下的有限强度单调性为对照基准；2) 指出相干量子动力学中，对最终目标有害的相干贡献可发生相消干涉，从轨迹层面改变损耗的作用符号；3) 导出轨迹级判据，判断有害贡献何时在物理上可被被动损耗接入并积累为净无条件收益；4) 将该判据编码为完全由无损耗动力学构造的响应算符：其谱决定弱被动损耗是否有益，其主特征向量识别最优环境模式；5) 在振动辅助传能模型中实例化并检验。

Result: 判据在振动辅助传能中识别出一个有益模式，该模式由两个各自有害的局域损耗方向相干组合而成；该预测模式在有限损耗下仍接近最优，相对独立优化的无损耗基准提升8.73%，而全有限损耗优化下为9.52%。

Conclusion: 相干量子动力学能够规避经典被动损耗单调性，被动损耗在量子情形下可成为提升无条件目标的资源。响应算符提供了仅依赖无损耗动力学、任务相关的设计原理，可用于构造有益的被动环境，避免昂贵的有限损耗全局优化。

Abstract: Passive loss removes probability from a system and therefore seems unable to improve an unconditional target. This intuition is exact for classical population dynamics under sink-only removal: passive killing obeys a finite-strength monotonicity principle even after optimization over the remaining controls. Here we show that coherent quantum dynamics can violate this classical monotonicity because coherent contributions to the final target can interfere destructively. We derive a trajectory-level criterion that determines when such a detrimental contribution is physically accessible to passive loss and accumulates into a net unconditional benefit. The criterion is encoded in a response operator constructed entirely from the lossless dynamics: its spectrum determines whether weak passive loss can help, while its principal eigenvector identifies the optimal environmental mode. Applying this criterion to vibronically assisted transfer, we identify a beneficial mode formed by coherently combining two individually detrimental local loss directions. The predicted mode remains near-optimal at finite loss, producing an \(8.73\%\) enhancement over the independently optimized lossless benchmark, compared with \(9.52\%\) under full finite-loss optimization. Our results establish how coherent quantum dynamics can evade classical passive-loss monotonicity and provide a general task-dependent principle for designing beneficial passive environments from lossless dynamics.

</details>


### [86] [Ancilla-free randomized Toffoli gates and the T-count of Boolean oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38189)
*Arul Rhik Mazumder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究容错Clifford+T电路中布尔oracle的T门与辅助量子比特开销，证明通过在各门的控制位上就地计算采样的奇偶性，可使随机化Toffoli门及仿射子空间指示函数的T-count与门规模无关且不再占用额外量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在容错Clifford+T电路中，Toffoli门和布尔oracle需要消耗T门和额外量子比特，且四比特及以上的精确Toffoli门各需一个额外量子比特。Gosset、Kothari与Zhang已证明对每次调用采样新的随机电路可使Toffoli门的T-count与其规模无关，但其采样器的直接实现每比特精度约需一个额外量子比特。本文旨在消除这一额外量子比特开销，并厘清随机化无测量电路与最优解的差距。

Method: 将采样的奇偶性就地计算在门自身的控制位上，而非引入辅助量子比特；分析此类随机电路在门规模足够大时的T门消耗；将结论推广到仿射子空间指示函数的异或（如等式检验与线性方程检验）；并将随机化无测量电路与最优下界、以及精确多控Toffoli门T-count的主常数问题相联系。

Result: 对足够大的门，就地计算可完全去除额外量子比特：每个随机电路每比特精度约需8个T门，仅借用门自身的空闲输入，比保留一个干净辅助比特的最佳无测量变体多1个T门。对不测量的随机电路，该方案在附加常数意义下与最优解相差不超过8倍；当每个随机电路精确计算布尔函数时，渐近闭合该因子等价于确定精确多控Toffoli门T-count的首项常数。此外，固定密度随机集合的指示函数在常数误差下需指数多个T门，而同密度仿射子空间的指示函数只需常数个，额外量子比特仅能将指数开销二次方地降低。

Conclusion: 就地奇偶计算可彻底消除大规模Toffoli门与仿射指示函数oracle的辅助量子比特需求，代价仅为每电路1个额外T门；所得随机化无测量实现已接近最优（因子8以内），并揭示了进一步改进与精确多控Toffoli门T-count首项常数之间的等价关系。随机集合与仿射子空间之间在T门复杂度上存在指数级差距。

Abstract: In fault-tolerant Clifford+T circuits, a Boolean oracle costs T gates and extra qubits, and on four or more qubits every exact Toffoli gate needs an extra qubit. Gosset, Kothari and Zhang showed that sampling a fresh random circuit for each call makes the T-count of the Toffoli gate independent of its size, but a direct implementation of their sampler uses about one extra qubit per bit of precision. We show that computing the sampled parities in place, on the gate's own controls, removes every extra qubit once the gate is large enough: each random circuit then uses about 8 T gates per bit of precision and borrows only the gate's own idle inputs. This is one T gate more per circuit than the best measurement-free variant that keeps one clean ancilla. The same holds for exclusive-ors of indicators of affine subspaces, such as equality tests and checks of linear equations. For randomized circuits that do not measure, this is within a factor of eight of optimal up to an additive constant, even with extra qubits, and when each random circuit computes a Boolean function exactly, closing the factor asymptotically is equivalent to determining the leading constant in the T-count of exact multi-controlled Toffoli gates. Finally, the oracle of the indicator of a random set of fixed density needs exponentially many T gates at constant error, while that of an affine subspace of the same density needs a constant number, and extra qubits reduce the exponential cost only quadratically.

</details>


### [87] [Barren Plateaus in Quantum Linear Combinations: Interference and Lie-Algebraic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38193)
*Amir Koutahi,Pouya Kananian,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究量子线路相干叠加（LCU）引入的干涉对训练信号的影响，区分单次LCU与交替分支-辅助混态结构，给出了方差下界、精确损失/梯度公式、可控性（生成李代数）刻画及浓度现象分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干叠加的量子线路（LCU）带来分支间干涉，会改变训练信号的统计性质，但此前缺乏对梯度方差下界、干涉额外项以及重复交错结构的系统性刻画。

Method: (1) 对单次LCU证明梯度方差下界：总方差≥某分支方差×其系数模的四次方，与门位置无关且不要求Haar随机；(2) 推导精确的损失与梯度公式，分离出干涉附加项，并指出依赖相对系数相位、在分支集对 i·I 乘法不变时消失的项，用四比特matchgate例子说明其非零；(3) 在辅助比特全特殊酉控制下，从分支的结合代数确定生成的李代数及中心相位方向，给出可控性结果；(4) 结合matchgate实例分析单次LCU多项式方差与联合Haar采样下指数浓度；(5) 给出系数的选取规则，以及后选择、测量误差与近似矩的界。

Result: 单次LCU的方差下界被严格证明（任意门位置、无需Haar假设）；干涉项存在依赖系数相位的非零贡献（四比特matchgate实例验证）；重复交错结构的生成李代数与中心相位被完全刻画，表明浓度结论需额外采样假设；matchgate实例呈现单次LCU方差多项式增长，而联合Haar采样下呈指数浓度；并给出后选择、测量误差与近似矩的界。

Conclusion: 相干叠加干涉会实质改变训练信号，但单次LCU的梯度方差不能被零矩阵均值分支消除；干涉附加项与相位结构相关，可精确量化。重复交错的表达能力由分支结合代数生成的李代数决定，因此浓度行为依赖采样假设，而非仅由结构决定。

Abstract: A coherent sum of quantum circuits introduces interference that can alter the training signal. We distinguish a single linear combination of unitaries (LCU) from a circuit that repeatedly alternates branch operations and ancilla mixing. For a single-pass LCU, independent branches with zero matrix means cannot erase the differentiated branch's gradient variance: the total variance is at least the branch variance times the fourth power of its coefficient magnitude. This bound holds at any gate position and does not require the circuit unitaries to be Haar-random. Beyond this lower bound, we derive exact loss and gradient formulas that quantify the additional contributions from interference between branches. One of these terms depends on relative coefficient phases and vanishes when the branch ensemble is invariant under multiplication by $i \mathbb{I}$; a four-qubit matchgate example shows that it can be nonzero. For repeated interleaving with full special-unitary ancilla control, we determine the generated Lie algebra from the associative algebra of the branches, including the available central phase directions. This control result predicts concentration only with additional assumptions on circuit sampling. Matchgate examples exhibit polynomial single-pass LCU variances and exponential concentration under joint Haar-random sampling. We also derive coefficient-selection rules and bounds for postselection, measurement error, and approximate moments.

</details>


### [88] [Noise Propagation and Effective Channels in Encrypted Quantum Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38240)
*Yun-Fei Li,Fei-Hao Yang,Qing Chen,Liu-Jun Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 以有效恢复信道刻画加密量子克隆中的噪声传播，发现有效恢复噪声不仅取决于噪声强度，还取决于物理位置与加解密协议结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清加密量子克隆协议中局部噪声如何经加解密流程传播到恢复态，是评估该协议实际可行性的关键，但此前缺乏对噪声位置依赖性的系统刻画。

Method: 将协议表述为有效恢复信道；对局域单量子比特CPTP噪声做Kraus算符的Pauli基展开，分辅助信号量子比特、辅助密钥量子比特与核心量子比特逐一定位分析，并讨论纠缠对数的宇称效应；同时把Werner型资源缺陷与局域退极化噪声相联系，区分分布式乘积退极化与整体全局退极化。

Result: 辅助信号量子比特上的噪声不改变恢复的约化态；辅助密钥量子比特上的噪声在恢复量子比特上诱导Pauli对角有效信道；核心量子比特的误差传播额外依赖纠缠对数的宇称，奇宇称下核心密钥量子比特噪声使不同物理Pauli扇区合并，各向同性退极化噪声转化为各向异性恢复信道。

Conclusion: 有效恢复噪声由噪声强度、物理位置及加解密协议结构共同决定，位置分辨的信道分析是评估加密量子克隆噪声鲁棒性的必要工具。

Abstract: We investigate noise propagation in encrypted quantum cloning by formulating the protocol in terms of effective recovery channels. For local single-qubit completely positive trace-preserving noise, a Pauli-basis expansion of the Kraus operators reveals a strong dependence on the location of the physical channel. Noise on an auxiliary signal qubit leaves the reduced recovered state unchanged, whereas noise on an auxiliary key qubit induces a Pauli-diagonal effective channel on the recovered qubit. For the core qubits, the error propagation additionally depends on the parity of the number of entangled pairs. In particular, for noise on the core key qubit with odd parity, distinct physical Pauli sectors merge into the same effective sector, converting isotropic depolarizing noise into an anisotropic recovery channel. We also relate Werner-type resource imperfections to local depolarizing noise and distinguish distributed product depolarization from genuine global depolarization. The results demonstrate that the effective recovery noise is governed not only by noise strength, but also by its physical location and by the structure of the encryption-decryption protocol.

</details>


### [89] [Active Approach to Fast Quantum State Preparation and Reset in Atomic Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38261)
*Ya-Tang Yu,Wei-Hsuan Chung,Tian-Shu Gou,Kuan-Ting Lin,H. H. Jen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在低激发波导耦合发射体阵列中，利用双正交模式分解揭示弛豫对亚辐射模重叠度的依赖（Mpemba效应的模式解释），并据此设计局域驱动协议加速基态重置与Dicke态制备，同时提出由非厄米趋肤效应导致的"Skin-Mpemba效应"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息处理需要快速制备目标态并重置到多体基态，但开放量子系统中慢衰减的亚辐射模式限制了弛豫与制备速度；作者希望从模式层面理解Mpemba效应并将其转化为可操作的加速控制策略。

Method: 将有效非厄米哈密顿量分解为双正交本征模式，分析弛豫与初始态在亚辐射模上重叠的关系；设计有限时长局域驱动以主动抑制慢衰减分量；提出先让一个发射体不被驱动、再恢复全驱动以稳定目标的两步Dicke态制备方案；并分析定向波导耦合下非厄米趋肤效应对集体模式边界的偏好。

Result: 在所研究参数下，局域驱动重置协议将到达固定残余激发阈值的时间较自由衰减缩短约25%；两步制备协议通过把谱权重重新分配到更亚辐射的模式，将Dicke态制备时间缩短约45%，且驱动能量消耗低于连续驱动；定向耦合使集体模式经非厄米趋肤效应向偏好边界移动。

Conclusion: 选择性局域控制可有效加速开放量子系统中的态制备与重置；弛豫动力学对系统手性的根本依赖被命名为"非厄米Skin-Mpemba效应"，同时为局域操作优选格点提供依据。

Abstract: Rapid preparation of a target quantum state and reset to the many-body ground state are essential for quantum information processing. Here, we investigate both processes in a waveguide-coupled emitter array in the low-excitation regime. By decomposing the effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian into biorthogonal eigenmodes, we show that relaxation depends on the initial overlap with the more subradiant modes, providing a modal explanation of the Mpemba effect. Guided by this insight, we design a reset protocol in which a finite-duration local drive activelysuppresses the relatively slow-decaying components. For the parameters studied, this reduces the time to reach a fixed residual-excitation threshold by up to approximately 25% compared with free decay. To prepare a target Dicke state, we introduce a two-step protocol that initially leaves one emitter undriven before restoring the full drive that stabilizes the target. This local control redistributes spectral weight into the more subradiant modes, driving them closer to their target amplitudes, which reduces the preparation time by up to approximately 45% while consuming less driving energy than continuous driving. Finally, we show how directional waveguide coupling shifts collective modes toward a preferred boundary through the non-Hermitian skin effect, providing guidance for selecting the preferred site for local operations. We term this fundamental dependence of the relaxation dynamics on the system's chirality the ``non-Hermitian Skin-Mpemba effect.'' These results demonstrate how selective local control can accelerate state preparation and reset in open quantum systems.

</details>


### [90] [Maximally-Matched Mixture Decoding for Correlated Atom Loss in Quantum LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38283)
*Jacob W. Murri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用双比特门中原子丢失的强相关性，提出最大匹配混合（MMM）解码器，在[[144,12,12]] Gross码上将逻辑错误率较丢失无感解码器降低约25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子平台中，可预示的原子丢失是主要噪声通道，且双比特门上的丢失高度相关——一个原子丢失时其门搭档往往也丢失。既有解码器未能充分利用这种相关性，故作者希望据此构建更优解码器。

Method: 针对[[144,12,12]] Gross码的校验子提取电路：给定丢失读出结果，将丢失量子比特按共享门构成的可能配对表示为一个图，其最大匹配承载了几乎全部条件概率质量。MMM解码器据此对每次读出构建加权混合的超图（仅在最大匹配对应的丢失场景上混合），再交由普通置信传播（BP）解码器处理。

Result: 在[[144,12,12]] Gross码上，MMM将逻辑错误率相对丢失无感解码器最多降低约25倍，相对Baranes等人（2026）的丢失感知均匀混合基线降低约2倍。

Conclusion: 通过图匹配刻画丢失相关性可显著提升中性原子量子纠错的解码性能，且该方法与标准BP解码器兼容，具备实用性。

Abstract: Heralded atom loss is a dominant noise channel in neutral atom quantum computing platforms, and losses at a two-qubit gate are strongly correlated: when one atom is lost, its gate partner is often lost too. This work shows how to exploit this correlation to construct a better decoder, focusing on [[144,12,12]] Gross code syndrome extraction circuits. Given a loss readout, the possible pairings of the lost qubits according to shared gates form a graph whose maximum matchings carry almost all of the conditional probability mass. Our maximal-matching-mixture (MMM) decoder builds a per-readout decoding hypergraph as a weighted mixture over exactly these loss scenarios. This decoding hypergraph can be used by an ordinary belief-propagation decoder. On the [[144,12,12]] Gross code, MMM lowers the logical error rate by as much as ~25x relative to a loss-unaware decoder and ~2x relative to a loss-aware uniform-mixture baseline from Baranes et al. (2026).

</details>


### [91] [Schur$\unicode{x2013}$Weyl Reduction of Finite-Copy Precision Bounds for Multiparameter Quantum Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38299)
*Jianchao Zhang,Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用Schur–Weyl对偶消去置换冗余，将i.i.d.多拷贝量子统计模型约化为多项式规模模型，并给出紧Cramér–Rao界的精确锥规划与NH松弛的半定规划表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数量子估计中不相容测量导致误差权衡，需理解有限次联合测量可达精度；但直接优化随张量积空间指数增长而代价高昂。

Method: 对i.i.d.拷贝，量子统计模型在置换下不变，借助Schur–Weyl对偶去掉置换重数空间并以维数作权重，得到与任意集体测量下原模型等价的约化统计模型；其上给出紧Cramér–Rao界的精确锥规划及NH松弛的SDP；用精确二次恒等式校正有限射线对偶解以得严格上下界，并进行网格收敛性分析。

Result: 固定单拷贝维数时约化算子表示随拷贝数多项式增长而非指数增长；固定拷贝数与参数维数时，足够细网格存在可行原始点，有界目标间隙的对偶解具有一致有界的紧搜索半径，收敛速度在射影覆盖半径上为二次；数值示例验证了网格修正项的二次缩放与约化NH的可计算范围。

Conclusion: Schur–Weyl对称约化使有限拷贝多参数量子估计的紧界与NH松弛在多项式资源下可计算，并具备可证明的网格收敛保证。

Abstract: In multiparameter quantum estimation, incompatible measurements can impose trade-offs among estimation errors. Understanding the precision attainable with a finite number of jointly measured copies is therefore important, but direct optimization becomes costly as the tensor product space grows exponentially. For independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) copies, the quantum statistical model is invariant under permutations of the copies. We exploit this symmetry through Schur--Weyl duality to remove permutation multiplicity spaces while using their dimensions as weights. This gives a reduced statistical model equivalent to the original under arbitrary collective measurements. On this reduced model, we derive an exact conic formulation of the tight Cramér--Rao bound and a semidefinite program for its Nagaoka--Hayashi (NH) relaxation. For fixed single-copy dimension, the reduced operator representation grows polynomially rather than exponentially with the number of copies. To approximate the tight bound, we use an exact quadratic identity to correct finite-ray dual solutions and obtain rigorous upper and lower bounds. For fixed copy number and parameter dimension, sufficiently fine meshes admit feasible primal points, and dual solutions with bounded objective gaps have uniformly bounded compact-search radii. These results yield quadratic convergence in the projective covering radius. Numerical examples illustrate the quadratic scaling of the mesh correction term and the computational reach of the reduced NH formulation.

</details>


### [92] [Strict Frequency Bandwidths from Causal Light Cones Enable Scalable Exact Gradient Evaluation in Quantum Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38322)
*Zhaoyang Song,Wei Zhang,Ji Cao,Xuexi Yi,Hong-Fu Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 浅层量子电路中的动力学局域性可被利用来减少精确梯度评估所需的电路设置数，从O(m)降到O(1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学局域性通常被视为浅层电路的局限；本文希望反过来将其视为计算资源，用于降低共享参数梯度精确评估的成本。

Method: 对由对易局域项生成的层状拟设，利用严格的海森堡光锥将变单一共享层参数所得能量切片限制在由局域因果几何决定的有限傅里叶带宽内；将精确梯度评估重构为局域信号恢复问题，在频率位于已知频率格上时，基于离散正弦变换导出精确多点参数移位规则；以QAOA为典型实例，用相互作用线图中的接触区域刻画几何结构。

Result: 在固定电路深度、有界度图上，评估相应导数所需的不同的电路设置数由O(m)降至O(1)。

Conclusion: 动力学局域性不仅是关联传播的约束，也是量子优化中实现更少电路设置下精确梯度评估的计算资源。

Abstract: Dynamical locality is often regarded as a limitation of shallow quantum circuits, yet we show that it can reduce the number of circuit settings required for exact evaluation of gradients with respect to shared parameters. For layered ansatze generated by commuting local terms, strict Heisenberg light cones confine energy slices obtained by varying one shared layer parameter to finite Fourier bandwidths dictated by local causal geometry. This bandlimit recasts exact gradient evaluation as a local signal recovery problem and, when the relevant frequencies lie on a known frequency lattice, yields an exact multipoint parameter-shift rule based on the discrete sine transform. The quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) provides a canonical realization in which this mechanism is governed by the local structure of the graph. Specifically, when a cost angle is varied, the relevant geometry is encoded by the contact region in the interaction line graph. At fixed circuit depth on graphs of bounded degree, the number of distinct circuit settings required for the corresponding derivative collapses from $O(m)$ to $O(1)$. This shows that dynamical locality is not merely a constraint on correlation propagation, but also a computational resource that enables exact gradient evaluation with fewer circuit settings in quantum optimization.

</details>


### [93] [Einstein Metrics and Quantum Control on Symmetric Tensor Product Quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38340)
*Honghuai Fang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明了量子门商流形 M_n 上恰有两个 PU(2^n)-不变 Einstein 度量（模缩放），并给出具有非负 Ricci 曲率、典型控制距离指数增长的度量的锐利曲率阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 n 量子比特投影酉群模局部酉变换与量子比特置换所得的商流形 M_n 上的不变 Einstein 度量，以刻画量子门代价几何与度量结构之间的关系。

Method: 在商流形 M_n=(PU(2)^n⋊S_n)\PU(2^n) 上研究 PU(2^n)-不变的 Einstein 方程；将两比特门代价归一化后讨论直径与控制距离；构造非 Einstein 度量并分析其 Ricci 曲率符号，确定曲率阈值。

Result: 对充分大的 n，M_n 上模缩放恰有两个 PU(2^n)-不变 Einstein 度量，二者在两比特代价归一化后直径一致有界；尽管逼近 Haar 随机酉算子需指数多两比特门。同时构造出具有非负 Ricci 曲率且典型控制距离指数增长的非 Einstein 度量，并给出该族度量的锐利曲率阈值。

Conclusion: M_n 的不变 Einstein 度量数量有限且几何直径有界，与 Haar 随机酉算子所需指数门数形成对比；非 Einstein、非负 Ricci 曲率度量可产生指数级控制距离，曲率阈值可精确确定。

Abstract: Let $M_n=(\mathrm{PU}(2)^n\rtimes S_n)\backslash\mathrm{PU}(2^n)$ be the space of projective $n$-qubit unitaries modulo local unitaries and permutations of the qubits. We prove that for all sufficiently large $n$ the manifold $M_n$ carries exactly two $\mathrm{PU}(2^n)$-invariant Einstein metrics up to scaling. Both Einstein metrics have uniformly bounded diameter after normalizing two-qubit costs, although approximating a Haar-random unitary requires exponentially many two-qubit gates. In contrast, we construct non-Einstein metrics with nonnegative Ricci curvature and exponentially growing typical control distances, and determine the sharp curvature threshold for this family.

</details>


### [94] [Symmetry-Preserving Hierarchical Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm with Subproblems Beyond the Qubit Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38344)
*Zhaoyang Song,Wei Zhang,Ji Cao,Xuexi Yi,Hong-Fu Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出对称保持分层QAOA（SP-HQAOA），通过宇称扇区收缩同时缓解比特数与电路深度瓶颈，并证明该归约的唯一性、给出确定性的修复风险界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子设备的比特数与可达电路深度是QAOA实用化的主要瓶颈；分治-合并可减少单电路编码变量数，基于粗粒化的收缩可扩展浅电路的有效作用范围，但已有工作未同时解决这两个问题。

Method: SP-HQAOA在每一步收缩中采用宇称扇区收缩，保持有效目标的零场Ising结构；证明此为唯一具备该性质的归约；逐次收缩使最大叶块规模B与每次量子调用比特上限Q解耦，任何量子电路最多用Q比特（即使B>Q）；并建立每次扇区限制所导致最优值损失的确定性修复风险界，该界可跨收缩步骤与递归层级求和。

Result: 深度p=1时，在N=40随机正则MaxCut叶块上达到精确最优的99.4%–99.7%；在每电路最多10比特下，N=500问题达到经典参考解的96%。

Conclusion: SP-HQAOA证明浅层量子电路可处理变量数远超单次量子调用比特上限的组合优化问题，有望在近期实现实用量子优化优势。

Abstract: The quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) is a paradigmatic variational quantum algorithm for combinatorial optimization problems, yet on current quantum devices the available qubit count and achievable circuit depth remain the main bottlenecks to its practical application. Divide-and-merge reduces the number of variables encoded in a single quantum circuit, while contraction based on coarse graining extends the effective range of shallow circuits, but existing work has not addressed both challenges simultaneously. We propose symmetry-preserving hierarchical QAOA (SP-HQAOA), in which parity sector contraction preserves the zero-field Ising structure of the effective objective at every contraction step, thereby addressing both within a single algorithm. We further prove that this reduction is the unique one with this property. Successive contractions further decouple the maximum leaf block size $B$ from the qubit limit $Q$ per quantum call, so every quantum circuit uses at most $Q$ qubits even when $B>Q$. We also establish a deterministic repair-risk bound on the possible loss in optimal value caused by each sector restriction, and the resulting bounds can be summed across contraction steps and levels of the recursive hierarchy. With depth $p=1$, SP-HQAOA attains $99.4\%$ to $99.7\%$ of the exact optimum on $N=40$ random regular MaxCut leaf blocks and, with at most $10$ qubits per circuit, $96\%$ of the classical reference for $N=500$. SP-HQAOA demonstrates the capacity to use shallow quantum circuits to address combinatorial optimization problems whose variable count far exceeds the qubit limit of a single quantum call, and may enable practical quantum optimization advantages in the near term.

</details>

Abstract: We theoretically investigate coherent anti-Stokes Raman scattering (CARS) driven by bright twin beams with sum-frequency generation (SFG) readout. </details>

*Xu Chen,Xue Ma*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We quantify the extra Bayes risk of locating a quantum change point and estimating its amplitude with one measurement. The joint and marginal optimizations use the full record and the same joint prior, with both parameters unknown. For product records on a known pure state curve, position has clipped absolute loss in resolution units, and amplitude offset has squared loss in fixed calibrated units. Ideal steps exhibit certified conflict at finite signal strength, including in phase records of sixteen qubits, and recover both informative marginal optima at strong signal. For finite offset prior width, a retained quantum boundary region serves position, and the remaining suffix serves amplitude. Physical recovery holds when strength grows more slowly than the square root of record length. Known smooth rises also admit leading recovery in the coherent experiment as strength grows and width shrinks. For the quintic profile, we establish two residual cost scaling laws. The residual cost is $Θ(s^{-2})$ as $s\to\infty$. Taking the strong signal limit at fixed $w$ yields a limiting cost of $Θ(w^3)$ as $w\to0$. In the resolved local model, quadratic position loss admits a common optimum within the covariant class. These results link onset geometry and decision loss to Bayesian measurement compatibility in quantum change point estimation.

</details>


### [97] [Spectral Reduction Selects the Observable Imprint of Dirac Quantum Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38379)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Spectral constraints can alter the observable manifestation of quantum geometry without changing the local eigenspace. Relativistic electron vortices provide a sharp example: the spin orbit interaction redistributes spin and orbital angular momentum, yet its magnetic contribution differs between localized three-dimensional wave packets and stationary monochromatic beams. We resolve this discrepancy by showing that fixing the energy replaces ambient momentum derivatives in position-weighted current moments by shell-tangent derivatives and derive a Hermitian selection rule for first-order observables in momentum-dependent internal subspaces. Its extension-independent form applies beyond Dirac systems, including a broad class of antisymmetric first moments of scalar-charge velocity currents. For the free positive-energy Dirac doublet, the magnetic correction is an exact non-Abelian Berry-curvature contraction with a pointwise saturable quantum-metric bound. Changing the relative phase between the two $σ_z$ spinor components switches this correction from zero in a conserved transverse-helicity sector to a finite value in a radial coherent superposition. At fixed finite Lorentz factor, the radial response scales as $O(θ/γ)$, compared with $O(θ^2)$ for longitudinal polarization. Spectral reduction and internal coherence thus provide distinct controls of the observable imprint of quantum geometry.

</details>


### [98] [Fermionic Unruh effect revisited: Entanglement and correlation dynamics under symmetric transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38387)
*Omid Yahyayi Monem,Hosein Mohammadzadeh*

TL;DR: 提出一种对称的类Unruh映射，用于描述加速观测者对Dirac场真空与单粒子态的双向振幅交换，并系统比较其对量子关联存续的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准处理下加速噪声表现为单侧振幅阻尼信道，导致纠缠与量子失协在无限加速极限仍存活；作者怀疑这一"存活"结论依赖于映射结构而非物理必然，故需构造更对称的信道来检验。

Method: 对Dirac场引入真空态与单粒子态同时与不可及Rindler区域交换振幅的对称Unruh型映射；对Bell态导出翻转概率等于Unruh温度下Fermi-Dirac占据数的比特翻转信道；用negativity、互信息及全部六种两两量子失协进行定量分析。

Result: 对称映射下，惯性观测者与可及模式间的纠缠和失协在无限加速极限同时趋于零，仅剩一比特经典关联；与标准振幅阻尼信道的"关联存活"结论形成鲜明对照。

Conclusion: 非惯性系中量子关联的命运取决于Minkowski-Rindler映射的具体结构，因而关于加速退相干的结论具有映射依赖性，需谨慎对待。

Abstract: A uniformly accelerated observer perceives the Minkowski vacuum as a thermal bath, so acceleration acts as a noise channel on correlations shared with an inertial partner. For a Dirac field in the standard treatment, the resulting map is a one-sided amplitude-damping channel, and entanglement and quantum discord survive at infinite acceleration. We introduce a symmetric Unruh-like map in which the vacuum and the one-particle state both exchange amplitude with the inaccessible Rindler region; for a Bell state it yields a bit-flip channel whose flip probability is the Fermi-Dirac occupation at the Unruh temperature. Using negativity, mutual information, and all six pairwise quantum discords, we find that the entanglement and discord between the inertial observer and the accessible mode vanish together at infinite acceleration, leaving only one bit of classical correlation. These results show that the fate of quantum correlations in noninertial frames depends on the structure of the Minkowski-Rindler map.

</details>


### [99] [Robustness of Magic and the Directed Geometry of State Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38401)
*Satoshi Kanno,Yoshi-aki Shimada*

TL;DR: 本文基于魔法鲁棒性（RoM）的对偶结构，引入由稳定子态标定的相空间距离，证明RoM恰可表示为到自由态集合的距离；并将该几何推广到态转换，定义有向转换缺陷C_F，用以刻画自由可转换性及更强的转换阻碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于RoM的魔法态资源下界只能给出单一标量度量，难以充分刻画态转换的可行性与阻碍；需要一种几何化、可刻画转换方向的工具来更精细地区分资源等价态的转换能力。

Method: 利用RoM的对偶结构构造以稳定子态标定的相空间距离，证明RoM等价于到自由态集合的距离；进而定义目标态与输入态自由可达集合之间的距离为有向转换缺陷C_F，并通过1+2C_F将其与表示转换残差所需的最小稳定子准概率权重相联系；对单量子比特系统解析构造RoM相同但可转换性不对称的混合态连续族。

Result: C_F可刻画自由可转换性，包含基于RoM的传统魔态资源下界，并能检测更强的转换阻碍；单量子比特情形下解析给出RoM相同但转换性非对称的混合态连续族。

Conclusion: 该工作以几何视角统一了RoM与态转换分析，提出的有向转换缺陷C_F是比传统RoM下界更精细的转换判据，并揭示了资源度量相同并不蕴含转换能力对称。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a state-space distance calibrated by stabilizer states based on the dual structure of the robustness of magic (RoM), and show that RoM is exactly represented as the distance to the free-state set. We then extend this geometry to state conversion by defining the directed conversion deficiency $C_{\mathcal F}$ as the distance between a target state and the set of states freely reachable from a given input state. The quantity $C_{\mathcal F}$ characterizes free convertibility and is related to the minimum stabilizer quasiprobability weight required to represent the conversion residual through the relation $1+2C_{\mathcal F}$. It also contains the conventional magic state resource lower bounds based on RoM and can detect stronger conversion obstructions. For single-qubit systems, we analytically construct a continuous family of mixed states with identical RoM but asymmetric convertibility.

</details>


### [100] [Controllability-Based Input Allocation in Quantum System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38415)
*Riku Yaegashi,Kazuhiro Sato,Kentaro Ohki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出面向薛定谔方程支配的量子输入-输出动力学的可控性分配评分（V-CAS），通过多可观测量量子控制Gramian将终端输出响应分解为各虚拟局域哈密顿扰动方向的通道贡献，并以单纯形约束的对数行列式优化给出最优虚拟输入强度分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解局域扰动如何影响全局量子动力学对大规模量子系统的分析与设计很重要，但现有量子中心性度量多依赖静态关联或纠缠，无法直接刻画幺正演化下的扰动传播。

Method: 沿名义轨迹引入虚拟局域哈密顿扰动，基于多可观测量量子控制Gramian把终端输出响应按通道分解；用单纯形约束的log-det优化组合各通道贡献定义V-CAS；刻画输出Gramian的值域与最小能量性质，给出保证评分在几乎每个可行终端时刻唯一性的漂移无关充分条件；针对多体系统用MPS+TEBD实现以避免显式构造指数规模状态转移矩阵，并推导Trotter与MPS截断误差传播到离散输出Gramian的误差界。

Result: 数值实验表明该评分捕捉到不同于静态关联和基于纠缠度量所识别的有限时间影响模式，并可指导与目标无关的控制通道预选。

Conclusion: V-CAS提供了一种刻画有限时间、依赖于输出与轨迹的局域扰动协同动力学影响力的可计算评分，适用于大规模量子系统的通道筛选与动力学分析。

Abstract: Understanding how local perturbations influence global quantum dynamics is important for analyzing and designing large-scale quantum systems. Existing quantum centrality measures often rely on static correlations or entanglement and do not directly capture perturbation propagation under unitary evolution. We formulate controllability allocation scoring for quantum input-output dynamics governed by the Schrodinger equation. Using the multiobservable quantum-control Gramian, we introduce virtual local Hamiltonian perturbations along a nominal trajectory and decompose the terminal-output response into channel-wise Gramian contributions. A simplex-constrained log-determinant optimization combines these contributions to define a volumetric controllability allocation score (V-CAS), specifying an optimal allocation of virtual input intensities among local Hamiltonian perturbation directions. The score measures their cooperative dynamical influence and depends on the output and trajectory. We characterize the output Gramian's range and minimum-energy properties and derive a drift-independent sufficient condition ensuring score uniqueness for almost every terminal time at which the problem is feasible. For quantum many-body systems, we develop a matrix-product-state and time-evolving-block-decimation implementation that avoids explicitly constructing exponentially large state-transition matrices. We also derive an error bound propagating Trotter and MPS-truncation errors to the discrete output Gramian. Numerical experiments show that the score captures finite-time influence patterns distinct from those identified by static correlation- and entanglement-based measures and can inform target-independent control-channel preselection.

</details>


### [101] [Harnessing Non-Markovian Memory for Quantum-Battery Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38527)
*Jun-Li Jiang,Xin-Zhu Liu,Xiu-Yong Ding,Xue Yang,Ming-Xing Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 从热力学视角探究非马尔可夫记忆能否作为操作性资源提升量子电池容量，并在过程张量框架下证明该记忆辅助容量优势对马尔可夫过程为零、且被非马尔可夫性的迹距离度量所界定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫性通常以信息论记忆特征刻画，但尚不清楚这种记忆能否被操作性地用于增强量子电池容量，因此作者转而采用热力学视角来检验记忆的资源价值。

Method: 在过程张量（process-tensor）框架中，对比无记忆与记忆增强两类策略，将二者性能差距定义为多时间过程的"记忆辅助容量优势"；给出该优势的界，并在连续时间设定下以随机电报退相位（random telegraph dephasing）模型与匹配的马尔可夫参考过程在同一控制类下进行比较。

Result: 证明该记忆辅助容量优势对马尔可夫过程张量恒为零，并由刻画过程非马尔可夫性的迹距离度量给出上界；连续时间情形下，演化中累积的记忆相对于匹配马尔可夫参考可在相同控制类下改善容量保持。

Conclusion: 非马尔可夫记忆是一种可用于量子电池容量的操作性资源，为记忆辅助的量子热力学任务提供了定量刻画与可检验判据。

Abstract: Non-Markovianity is commonly characterized through information-theoretic signatures of memory. Here we take a thermodynamic perspective and ask whether such memory can be operationally harnessed to enhance quantum-battery capacity. Within the process-tensor framework, we compare memoryless and memory-enhanced strategies and define their performance gap as the memory-assisted capacity advantage of a multitime process. We prove that this advantage vanishes for Markovian process tensors and is bounded by a trace-distance measure of process non-Markovianity. In a complementary continuous-time setting, we show using random telegraph dephasing that memory accumulated during the evolution can enhance capacity retention relative to a matched Markovian reference under the same control class. These results identify non-Markovian memory as an operational resource for quantum-battery capacity.

</details>


### [102] [Coherent errors, syndrome extraction and the Robustness of Magic in stabiliser codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38554)
*Enrico Bozzetto,Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文用Robustness of Magic量化相干错误对稳定子码编码魔态的影响：可纠正区域内不支撑稳定子的幺正错误U会使编码态稳健度等于逻辑态稳健度加一份U的Choi态稳健度；症候提取恰好移除这份附加魔量并回到逻辑值，且对每个非Clifford错误该移除的魔量均正。进而给出两比特门魔容量的Choi态表示、Clifford错误使症候提取测量逻辑算符的线性代数判据，以及Pauli噪声下存储魔态的魔寿命，并展示选择存储平面可使旋转表面码在低码容量去极化噪声下寿命延长约1.5倍，最后在奇素数维给出Wigner负性与稳健度寿命相等的态与信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干错误不像Pauli错误那样只落在单一症候扇区，而是使编码魔态处于症候扇区的叠加中，因此"相干错误会增添多少魔量"与"症候提取是否会消耗逻辑魔量"这两个基本问题缺乏定量答案；同时需要把门的魔容量、错误诊断、存储魔寿命等与具体码无关的量用同一度量统一刻画。

Method: 以Robustness of Magic（稳健度）为主度量，研究作用于可纠正区域、且该区域不支撑任何稳定子的幺正错误U，推导编码态稳健度与逻辑态稳健度及U的Choi态稳健度之间的精确关系；分析症候提取对该附加份的移除；利用"给任意态添加非稳定子态使(1+R)乘以严格大于1的紧界因子"这一性质判断被移除魔量的正性；对Clifford等价于对角门或单比特门的两比特门计算其魔容量；构造Clifford错误使症候提取等效测量逻辑算符的线性代数判据；在Pauli噪声下计算单比特魔态的魔寿命，并比较旋转表面码中不同存储平面；在奇素数维构造Wigner负性与稳健度寿命一致的态与信道。

Result: 对上述幺正错误U，在所有稳定子编码中编码态稳健度恰等于逻辑态稳健度加一份U的Choi态稳健度；症候提取移除该份后恢复逻辑稳健度；对每个非Clifford错误，被移除的魔量为正，且添加非稳定子态使(1+R)乘以严格大于1且可紧界因子；Clifford等价于对角或单比特门的两比特门，其魔容量等于其Choi态稳健度；给出Clifford错误导致症候提取测量逻辑算符的线性代数判据；在低码容量去极化噪声下，优化存储平面可使旋转表面码的魔寿命延长约1.5倍；在奇素数维找到Wigner负性与稳健度寿命相等的态与信道。

Conclusion: 相干错误引入的魔量可完全由错误的Choi态稳健度刻画，并与码的编码方式无关；症候提取是一次"净化"过程，其移除的魔量对任何非Clifford错误都严格为正，因而逻辑魔量与症候扇区相干性之间存在定量分离；稳健度可统一评估门魔容量、错误诊断与存储魔寿命，并可用于在具体码与噪声下做优化（如存储平面选择）以及比较不同非稳定度量（Wigner负性与稳健度）的寿命。

Abstract: Coherent errors leave stabiliser-code-encoded magic states in superpositions of syndrome sectors. We ask how much magic these coherent errors add, and whether syndrome extraction depletes logical magic. Using Robustness of Magic, we show that, for a unitary error U on a correctable region which supports no stabiliser, the encoded state has the same robustness as the logical state plus one copy of the Choi state of U , in every stabiliser code encoding. Syndrome extraction removes this copy, returning the logical robustness. The removed magic is positive for every non-Clifford error: adding a non-stabiliser state to any state multiplies one plus the robustness by a (tightly- bounded) factor above one. The magic capacity of two-qubit gates Clifford-equivalent to diagonal or single-qubit gates is equal to their Choi-state robustness. We give a linear-algebraic test for when a Clifford error makes syndrome extraction measure a logical operator, and give magic lifetimes of stored single-qubit magic states under Pauli noise. Choosing the plane of the stored state extends these lifetimes about 1.5x on rotated surface codes under low code-capacity depolarising noise. We identify states and channels in odd-prime dimension for which Wigner negativity and robustness show equal lifetimes.

</details>


### [103] [Operator quantum singular value transformation: Spectral mapping, design and topological invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09007)
*Chusei Kiumi,Yohei Tanaka,Akito Suzuki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文将量子奇异值变换(QSVT)从矩阵推广到可能无限维算符(operator QSVT),证明其通过QSP多项式作用而非SVD来变换算符各块,并建立谱映射定理、谱设计与拓扑保护理论,统一了Szegedy行走与split-step行走两类量子行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QSVT是量子算法的统一框架,但其谱性质、与凝聚态谱分析/能带设计的联系,以及常规QSVT中被舍弃的kernel sector的物理意义尚不明确。作者希望把QSVT与凝聚态的谱分析、谱设计与拓扑保护联系起来。

Method: 将QSVT推广为operator QSVT,作用于可能无限维算符的块编码;无需奇异值分解,用QSP多项式变换算符每一块;建立从嵌入算符经QSP多项式到QSVT算符全谱(含连续谱)的谱映射定理;利用谱映射进行谱设计(相位可拉伸、折叠、分离能带并放置本征值);分析kernel sector的拓扑指标及其在QSP相位选择、局域扰动与保隙形变下的不变性。

Result: operator QSVT对算符每一块施加QSP多项式变换且无需SVD;谱映射定理给出含连续谱的完整谱;QSP相位可实现能带拉伸、折叠与分离及本征值布置;kernel sector携带拓扑指标,在QSP相位、局域扰动和保隙形变下不变,保证拓扑保护的态。两类行走的kernel sector分别编码图的欧拉示性数与体绕数之差;对split-step行走,QSP破坏手征对称性但不破坏拓扑保护,并可将受保护态移入连续带形成BIC。

Conclusion: operator QSVT把量子算法中的Szegedy行走与凝聚态中的split-step行走统一起来,将QSP赋予谱分析、谱设计与拓扑保护三重功能,并揭示BIC等新现象。

Abstract: Quantum singular value transformation (QSVT) provides a unifying framework for quantum algorithms by applying the polynomials of quantum signal processing (QSP) to block-encoded matrices. We connect this framework with spectral analysis, design and topological protection in condensed matter physics. Extending QSVT beyond matrices to possibly infinite-dimensional operators (operator QSVT), we establish the following. First, it transforms every block of the operator by the QSP polynomials; no singular value decomposition is needed. Second, a spectral mapping theorem determines the full spectrum of the QSVT operator, including continuous spectra, from the embedded operator through the QSP polynomial, allowing spectral analysis without translation invariance. Third, the spectral mapping turns QSP into spectral design: the QSP phases can stretch, fold and separate the bands and place the eigenvalues. Finally, the kernel sectors that conventional QSVT usually sets aside carry a topological index, invariant under the choice of QSP phases, local perturbations and gap-preserving deformations, which guarantees topologically protected states. These results unify two quantum walks: Szegedy's walk from quantum algorithms and the split-step walk from condensed matter physics. Their kernel sectors encode the graph's Euler characteristic and the difference of bulk winding numbers, respectively. For the split-step walk, QSP breaks chiral symmetry but not the topological protection, and can move the protected state into a continuous band to form a bound state in the continuum (BIC).

</details>


### [104] [Learning Grover mixers for constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38572)
*Matteo Vandelli,Francesco Ferrari,Daniele Dragoni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出两阶段变分方法：先训练参数化量子电路制备可行态上的均匀叠加（熵型代价函数），再将其嵌入GM-QAOA提供初态与Grover混频器；在MWIS上稳定优于标准QAOA，在MKP上增益边际且随规模增大而消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GM-QAOA是约束组合优化的有力框架，但其实际落地依赖高效制备可行态上的均匀叠加，这是该方法的主要瓶颈。

Method: 两阶段变分方案：第一阶段用熵-based 代价函数（偏好可行比特串上的均匀分布）训练参数化量子电路以制备目标叠加态；第二阶段把训练好的电路作为GM-QAOA的初态，并与它的逆共同构成Grover混频器。在最大权重独立集（MWIS）与多维背包问题（MKP）上做基准评估。

Result: MWIS上相对标准QAOA取得一致改进；MKP上仅见边际增益，且随问题规模增大优势递减。MKP表现不佳归因于变分混频器训练难度上升，优化景观愈发复杂。

Conclusion: 结果体现了变分Grover混频器的潜力，同时揭示其性能增益与训练所需额外资源之间的权衡。

Abstract: Grover-Mixer QAOA (GM-QAOA) is a promising quantum framework for constrained combinatorial optimization. However, its practical implementation requires the efficient preparation of a uniform superposition over feasible states. In this work, we propose a two-stage variational approach. First, a parameterized quantum circuit is trained to prepare this superposition, using an entropy-based cost function that favors a uniform distribution over feasible bitstrings. The trained circuit is then embedded in GM-QAOA, where it provides both the initial state and, together with its inverse, yields the Grover mixer. The resulting method is evaluated on two representative benchmark problems: the Maximum Weight Independent Set (MWIS) problem and the Multidimensional Knapsack Problem (MKP). While the results demonstrate consistent improvements over standard QAOA for MWIS, only marginal gains are observed for MKP, with the advantage diminishing as the problem size increases. The reduced performance on MKP reflects the growing difficulty of training the variational mixer, likely due to increasingly challenging optimization landscapes. Overall, these findings highlight both the potential of variational Grover mixers and the trade-off between their performance gains and the additional resources required for their training.

</details>


### [105] [Pulse-Informed Physical Placement for Parallel Two-Qubit Gates in Qubit Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38604)
*Shahid Qamar,Wei-Feng Zhuang,Bilal Riaz,Peng Qian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将并行两比特门的物理放置与脉冲合成建模为耦合决策问题，提出QMDM表示与脉冲反馈的放置搜索，在合成3×3处理器上中位F_proj达0.9938，且QOCT迭代次数比穷举节省59.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行两比特层可由多组互不重叠的物理耦合器实现，但孤立标定无法反映各实现在自身同时控制脉冲优化后的真实表现，需在放置与脉冲之间建立联合评估。

Method: 针对异构频率可调transmon阵列中的并行CZ门，构建处理器感知的结构化量子多域矩阵（QMDM），将静态处理器、边、关联与任务信息与动态候选响应块相连；对每个候选放置P实例化有效哈密顿量H_P，由QOCT生成该放置专属脉冲，经直接耦合传播得到投影计算子空间保真度F_proj与计算生存率，并回馈QMDM对物理放置排序与渐进提升（脉冲知情放置）。

Result: 30个留出合成3×3处理器上，脉冲知情放置中位F_proj为0.993826，标定式放置为0.984981；在不同系统条件下，候选QOCT优化器迭代次数比穷举有限预算参考少59.1%。

Conclusion: 脉冲反馈的放置搜索显著优于标定式放置且大幅降低优化开销；QMDM表示与外层搜索可推广到任意L个并行两比特门，当前全局候选排序验证限于L=2。

Abstract: A parallel two-qubit layer can often be realized with several nonoverlapping sets of physical couplers, but isolated calibrations do not reveal how each realization performs after optimizing its own simultaneous control pulse. We formulate physical placement and pulse synthesis as a coupled decision problem for parallel controlled-$Z$ gates in heterogeneous frequency tunable transmon arrays. The proposed processor-aware structural quantum multi-domain matrix (QMDM) links static processor, edge, incidence, and task information to a dynamic candidate response block. For each candidate placement $P$, QMDM instantiates the active Hamiltonian $H_P$; quantum optimal control (QOCT) generates a placement-specific pulse, direct coupled propagation returns the projected computational subspace overlap $F_{\rm proj}$ and computational survival; and these pulse-conditioned responses are fed back to QMDM to rank and progressively promote physical placements. This placement pulse response feedback defines pulse-informed physical placement. Here, to see the performance of the proposed method on 30 held-out synthetic $3\times3$ processors, the median final $F_{\rm proj}$ is $0.993826$ for pulse-informed placement and $0.984981$ for calibration-based placement. Also, when tested under different system conditions, the proposed pulse-informed approach uses $59.1\%$ fewer candidate QOCT optimizer iterations than the exhaustive finite-budget reference. The QMDM representation and outer search are formulated for arbitrary $L$ simultaneous two-qubit gates. Here, the present global candidate ranking validation is used for $L=2$.

</details>


### [106] [Fate of Quantum Magic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39059)
*Liuke Lyu,Erik S. Sørensen,William Witczak-Krempa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个量化多体态"魔力"(magic,一种超越纠缠的量子资源)的框架,并将其应用于热化、淬火动力学、空间分离及实验探测等场景,发现魔力普遍存在"突然死亡"现象,且其死亡时序与纠缠的死亡时序不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stabilizer态可被高效经典描述却可具备最大纠缠,故量子优势必须依赖魔力;而多体态中魔力的定量评估及其在热化与动力学下的演化规律此前缺乏系统框架。

Method: 发展一个计算一般多体态魔力程度的通用框架;在横场Ising模型、一般自旋链及蜂窝格Kitaev量子自旋液体中研究热态魔力随温度与子系统尺寸的行为;研究淬火后魔力在子系统中的平衡化;引入非局域魔力的概念研究随分离距离的演化;并基于中子散射提出实验见证"帧稳健魔力"(frame-robust magic)体积律的方法。

Result: 横场Ising模型中魔力出现热"突然死亡",饱和值与系统尺寸相关,且发生在纠缠死亡之后;一般自旋链与Kitaev自旋液体中结论类似,后者阈值对子系统形状有复杂依赖。淬火情形下,一般自旋链子系统的魔力通常先于纠缠死亡,与热轨迹相反,可由非魔力态集合的几何解释。分离距离下非局域魔力亦呈现突然死亡。并给出利用中子散射实验见证帧稳健魔力体积律的方案。

Conclusion: 魔力作为量子资源在多体演化中遵循可定量刻画的"突然死亡"规律,其与纠缠的死亡次序依赖于轨迹类型(热态vs淬火),本文框架为多体魔力的理论与实验研究提供了通用工具。

Abstract: Magic, a quantum resource distinct from entanglement, measures departure from states stabilized by products of Pauli operators. Such stabilizer states can be maximally entangled yet admit efficient classical description: quantum advantage requires magic. We develop a powerful framework to evaluate the degree of magic in generic many-body states. We use it to study the fate of magic under thermal and dynamical evolution. In the transverse field Ising model, we find a thermal sudden death of magic that saturates with system size, and occurs after the death of entanglement. We reach similar conclusions in generic spin chains, and in the Kitaev quantum spin liquid on the honeycomb lattice, where the threshold shows an intricate dependence on subregion shape. We then study how magic equilibrates after a quantum quench. In quenches of generic spin chains, the magic of a subregion typically dies before entanglement, contrary to thermal trajectories. An explanation is given in terms of the geometry of non-magical states. As a third type of trajectory, we examine the fate of magic with separation with an appropriate notion of non-local magic and establish a sudden death. Finally, we show how to experimentally witness a volume law of frame-robust magic in quantum materials by using neutron scattering.

</details>


### [107] [Competition and cancellation in a tilted Rice--Mele chain: exact Wannier--Stark offset and spectral readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38630)
*Xiang Gao,Xing Deng,Shoulong Chen,Ying Zhang,Chunhua Zhang,Wei Xiao,Xueting Fang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了倾斜Rice–Mele链中交错Wannier–Stark阶梯的偏移量\(A\)，给出强场级数与Floquet精确闭式解，并据此提出多种无需时间演化的谱学判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倾斜周期链的WS阶梯理想等距，但交错在位能与二聚化跃迁使阶梯变为\(E_n=n\Fo+(-1)^{n+1}A\)。需要定量确定偏移\(A\)、弄清其归零点与谱等距性的关系，并寻找可实验读取的签名。

Method: 在WS基下做系统的强场级数展开，借助精确矩阵元将其化为Bessel代数；同时构造非微扰两带Floquet传播子，得到\(A=(\Fo/\pi)\arcsin(-i\,\mathrm{Tr}\,U/2)\)的闭式表达；并分析奇长度链的隐对称缺陷及体态边带合并。

Result: 代表性工作点下七阶截断可将归零点定位到理论内截断误差0.010 Hz（一阶为27.3 Hz）；Floquet结果在两带模型内严格，给出运动学界\(|A|\le\Fo/2\)，并将归零等同于最大谱均匀性；解析结构给出一族实零点与复例外点，界定强场级数收敛半径而不触及实轴；奇长链上出现高度\(2|A|\)的配对平台；体态中归零表现为两侧带并入\(\Fo\)处单线。

Conclusion: 交错WS阶梯的\(A\)可解析且精确地刻画，其归零对应最大谱均匀性；谱学签名（平台、侧带合并）可静态读取偏移，为校准感知的精密力传感提供互补检验路径。

Abstract: In a tilted Rice--Mele chain, the Wannier--Stark (WS) ladder becomes staggered, \(E_n=n\Fo+(-1)^{n+1}A\). The offset \(A\) reflects the competition between the staggered onsite energy \(Δ\) and the dimerized hopping \(\dt\), and its cancellation restores spectral equidistance. We determine \(A\) through a systematic strong-field series in the WS basis and a nonperturbative two-band Floquet expression. An exact matrix element reduces the strong-field expansion to Bessel algebra; at the representative working point, truncation at seventh order locates the cancellation point with a theory-internal truncation error of \(0.010\)~Hz, against \(27.3\)~Hz at first order. The Floquet propagator gives the closed form \(A=(\Fo/π)\arcsin(-i\,\mathrm{Tr}\,U/2)\), exact within the two-band model, implying the kinematic bound \(|A|\le\Fo/2\) and identifying cancellation with maximum spectral uniformity. The same analytic structure yields a family of real zeros and complex exceptional points that set the convergence radius of the strong-field series without touching the real axis. On odd-length chains, the hidden-symmetry defect produces a pairing plateau of height \(2|A|\) that persists numerically beyond first order in the modulations, allowing the offset to be read from the static spectrum without Floquet propagation or time evolution. In the bulk, cancellation appears as the merging of two sidebands into a single line at \(\Fo\), a null measurement that requires no calibrated sideband amplitudes, although the tilt and line frequency still need calibration. These signatures provide complementary spectral tests of the staggered WS ladder and a calibration-aware route to precision force sensing.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum Gate Engineering in Superconducting NISQ Platforms: Experimental Methods and Emerging Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38635)
*Alessandro Sarno,Viviana Stasino,Antonio Pio Italiano,Alessandra Pizza,Carlo Cosenza,Wladislav Guarino,Domenico Montemurro,Francesco Tafuri,Davide Massarotti,Halima Giovanna Ahmad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统梳理超导NISQ器件中量子门的实现路径，架起量子线路理论综合与实验落地之间的桥梁，涵盖器件架构、脉冲工程、校准流程与基准测试，服务于软硬件协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论层面的量子门/量子线路综合与实验层的真实硬件实现之间存在明显鸿沟，研究者、实验人员与量子工程师缺少一份兼顾技术细节与整体视角的、及时更新的参考资料。

Method: 综述性方法：系统考察从常规transmon到非常规设计的各类超导量子比特与处理器架构，评估其设计权衡、连通性约束、器件物理与材料影响；阐述单/双比特操作如何通过微波驱动与磁通激活脉冲合成，并给出脉冲工程、波形整形与校准工作流；采用真值表与量子过程层析等基准测试方法进行验证，并按具体评估维度选择更高效的度量。

Result: 构建了从抽象门模型到实际硬件限制的软硬件协同设计框架，将门合成理论与最基本门操作的实验实现结合，提出可操作的实用工作流。

Conclusion: 该综述为超导量子门实现提供了理论—实验贯通的技术性视角，强调脉冲工程、校准与基准测试的协同，并以软硬件协同设计为最终导向。

Abstract: This review aims to provide researchers, experimentalists, and quantum engineers with an updated and technically grounded perspective on the implementation of quantum gates in superconducting NISQ devices. The goal is to establish a comprehensive bridge between theoretical quantum circuit synthesis and its practical, experimental implementation, focusing on superconducting quantum technologies. We systematically examine the diverse landscape of superconducting qubits and processor architectures, ranging from conventional transmons to alternative and unconventional designs, evaluating their unique design trade-offs, connectivity constraints, device physics, and materials impact. Moving from theory to actual implementation, we detail how single- and two-qubit operations are synthesized via microwave-driven and flux-activated pulses. We here present our pulse engineering, wave shaping, calibration workflows, demonstrating how to bridge the gap between abstract gate models and real-world hardware limitations in the direction of a hardware-software co-design framework. To validate these operations, we choose benchmarking methodologies with the purpose to get the more efficient metric for the specific evaluation aspect, including truth tables and quantum process tomography. Therefore, we both discuss the theoretical concepts behind the gate synthesis and show experimental implementation of most fundamental gates by proposing a practical workflow.

</details>


### [109] [Hyperentanglement-enabled Quantum Privacy Amplification for Classical-Quantum Coexistence in Fiber Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38645)
*Domenico Ribezzo,Giulia Guarda,Alessandro Zavatta,Davide Bacco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用能量-时间与频率超纠缠实现单拷贝纠缠蒸馏，在物理层直接完成量子隐私放大，将QKD对经典激光入纤功率的容忍度提升4 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信需与光纤中高功率经典光信号共存，自发拉曼散射（SpRS）产生的宽带光子背景会劣化量子态，在QKD中甚至完全抑制安全密钥生成，因此需在物理层抑制该类噪声。

Method: 基于能量-时间与频率超纠缠实现单拷贝纠缠蒸馏；利用自发参量下转换的严格频率反关联，选择性丢弃不相关拉曼光子而保留真实纠缠对；采用无源硼硅玻璃光子集成芯片与标准DWDM解复用器，在20 km单模光纤测试平台上开展实验。

Result: 偶然符合计数被抑制为原来的1/N（N为频率通道对数）；执行QPA后，对经典激光入纤功率P_C的容忍度提升4 dB，安全密钥阈值由P_C=-13.3 dBm扩展至-9.3 dBm。

Conclusion: 现成商用电信器件可被复用为物理层量子隐私放大器，为量子与经典信号共纤传输提供可行途径。

Abstract: Integrating quantum communication into existing fiber infrastructures requires coexisting with high-power classical traffic, which generates noise in the quantum channel. Spontaneous Raman Scattering (SpRS) is the dominant noise source, generating a broadband photon background that degrades quantum states and, in Quantum Key Distribution (QKD), can entirely suppress secure key generation. In this work, we implement single-copy entanglement distillation utilizing energy-time and frequency hyperentanglement, and we exploit it to perform Quantum Privacy Amplification (QPA) directly at a physical layer. Strict frequency anti-correlations of spontaneous parametric down-conversion are used to selectively discard uncorrelated Raman photons while preserving genuine entangled pairs, suppressing accidental coincidences by a factor $1/N$, where $N$ is the number of frequency channel pairs. Using passive borosilicate-glass photonic integrated circuits and standard DWDM demultiplexers over a 20~km single-mode fiber testbed, we demonstrate a 4~dB improvement in tolerance to classical laser launch power $P_C$ when QPA is performed, extending the secure key threshold from $P_C=-13.3$ dBm to $P_C=-9.3$ dBm. The result shows that off-the-shelf telecom components can be repurposed as physical-layer quantum privacy amplifiers.

</details>


### [110] [Probing fluctuations in photon emission statistics of spin injected quantum dots with time dependent electronic excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40593)
*Chiran Wijesundara,Vincent Iglesias-Cardinale,Yuan Lu,Herbert Fotso,Bo Xu,Tim Thomay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 电驱动InGaAs/GaAs量子点通过CoFeB/MgO自旋注入层实现零磁场下的圆偏振单光子发射，并利用四探测器HBT装置测得单激发周期内分辨至|2⟩态的光子数统计；双光子统计的显著涨落随自旋注入层退磁而消失，表明自旋注入同时调控光子螺旋度与高阶时间关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建无需外部泵浦激光、与微电子工艺兼容的电驱动圆偏振量子光源，并厘清自旋极化载流子注入是否不仅决定光子螺旋度，还影响发射的高阶时间关联（多光子动力学），为自旋调控的Fock态源提供物理依据。

Method: 采用具有垂直磁各向异性的铁磁CoFeB自旋注入层与MgO隧穿势垒，将自旋极化电子注入InGaAs/GaAs量子点有源区；用四探测器、时间标记读出的扩展Hanbury Brown–Twiss干涉仪测量时间分辨光子数统计；通过自旋注入层退磁作对照实验；并用包含可变激发机制与多发射体collapse算符的随机波函数演化进行唯象建模。

Result: 零外加磁场下获得圆偏振单光子发射；时间分辨统计可分辨单激发周期内至|2⟩的Fock态；单光子（n=1）发射如预期跟随电激发脉冲，而双光子（n=2）统计在脉冲内多个时间位置出现明显涨落，退磁后该涨落消失，确立多光子发射动力学与注入载流子自旋极化的直接关联。

Conclusion: 自旋注入不仅控制发射光子的螺旋度，还控制其高阶时间关联；该平台为电驱动、自旋可控的单光子与多光子Fock态源开辟路径，并为固态量子光源中自旋条件下的多发射体动力学提供测试平台。

Abstract: Electrically spin-injected ensembles of InGaAs/GaAs quantum dots emit circularly polarized single photons, with the emission helicity determined by the spin polarization of the injected electrons. A ferromagnetic CoFeB spin-injection layer with perpendicular magnetic anisotropy, combined with an MgO tunnel barrier, enables spin-polarized electron injection into the quantum-dot active region, resulting in circularly polarized photon emission at zero applied magnetic field. These devices realize direct spin-charge-photon conversion in an electrically driven, microelectronics-compatible quantum light source that operates without an external pump laser. Using an extended Hanbury Brown-Twiss interferometer with four detectors and time-tagged readout, we measure time-resolved photon-number statistics that resolve up to $|2\rangle$ Fock states within a single excitation cycle. While single-photon ($n=|1\rangle$) emission tracks the electrical excitation pulse as expected, the two-photon ($n=|2\rangle$) statistics exhibit pronounced fluctuations at multiple temporal positions within the pulse. These fluctuations vanish upon demagnetization of the spin-injection layer, establishing a direct link between the multi-photon emission dynamics and the spin polarization of the injected carriers. The observations indicate that spin injection controls not only the helicity of the emitted photons but also higher-order temporal correlations of the emission. The behavior is phenomenologically captured by inserting both a variable excitation mechanism and a set of multi-emitter collapse operators within a stochastic wave function evolution. The platform thus opens a route to electrically driven, spin-controlled single and multi-photon Fock-state sources and provides a testbed for spin-conditioned multi-emitter dynamics in solid-state quantum light sources.

</details>


### [111] [Homodyne versus heterodyne detection for classifying families of optical quantum states from finite records of detection events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38671)
*Franciszek Grzywa,Sebastian Dolata,Karol Bartkiewicz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文比较有限探测记录下heterodyne与homodyne两种探测方案对七类光量子态（含GKP态）的识别性能，发现卷积网络下heterodyne在N=100、20%退相位时优于经滤波反投影的homodyne达14.1个百分点，但该差异在更大记录数下不显著，且源于重建流程而非homodyne本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络可从相空间函数图像识别量子态，但实际测量只能给出有限N个探测事件；heterodyne直接采样Husimi Q函数，homodyne需多相位重建才能得到Wigner函数，两种方案在有限记录条件下孰优孰劣尚不清楚。

Method: 在损耗、退相位和高斯噪声下，对七类量子态（含GKP态）构造两组态族（等平均光子数组、Ahmed等编码组），模拟有限记录；用卷积网络比较heterodyne与基于滤波反投影重建的homodyne；另做无重建的homodyne网络、似然比观察者对照，并在近热态处做解析（主阶）分析。

Result: N=100、20%退相位下，第一组中heterodyne较重建homodyne高14.1个百分点（24格校正p=0.003）；N=10³与10⁴时差异不显著但置信区间宽；N=100时无重建homodyne与heterodyne无显著差异（第一组），第二组名义上更差。近热态解析显示heterodyne对n阶相空间结构指数衰减，而homodyne在n+1个角向分量间分摊单位权重，属局部结果、不能解释有限记录差异。GKP态、光子减压缩态及EPR单模的实验记录分类表现与模拟一致，但低功率测试仅检验计数噪声模型。似然比观察者在N=100时无此差距。

Conclusion: heterodyne在有限记录下优于homodyne的优势局限于基于重建的流程，不代表homodyne探测本身更差；该结论对重建方式、相位数与记录规模敏感。

Abstract: Neural networks can identify an optical quantum state from an image of its phase-space function, but a real measurement gives a finite record of $N$ detection events. Heterodyne detection samples the Husimi $Q$ function directly, whereas homodyne detection gives the Wigner function only after a reconstruction from several phases. We ask which scheme better identifies one of seven families of states, including Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) states, under loss, dephasing and Gaussian noise. We simulate finite records on identical states for two sets of families, one with equal mean photon number and one built from the code of Ahmed et al. For convolutional networks, heterodyne detection outperforms homodyne detection with filtered back-projection by 14.1 percentage points at $N=100$ events and 20% dephasing in the first set (adjusted $p=0.003$ over $24$ cells across training runs), and it depends on the number of phases and on the reconstruction. At $N=10^3$ and $10^4$ no difference is significant, but the intervals are wide. At $N=100$, a homodyne network without reconstruction does not differ significantly from heterodyne detection in the first set and is nominally worse in the second. Near thermal states we show to leading order that heterodyne detection damps phase-space structure of order $n$ exponentially, whereas homodyne detection shares unit weight among $n+1$ angular components, a local result that does not explain the finite-record differences. Experimental records of a GKP state, photon-subtracted squeezed states and one mode of an Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen source are classified like simulated ones, but these low-power tests probe our counting-noise model, not the seven-class accuracy. A likelihood-ratio observer shows no such gap at $N=100$, so our evidence concerns a reconstruction-based pipeline, not homodyne detection in general.

</details>


### [112] [Exceptional Points as Witnesses of Non-Gaussian Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40612)
*Aritra Ghosh,Subhashish Banerjee,M. Bhattacharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在噪声平均的退相干响应中出现异常点（EP），可作为非高斯统计的可观测特征；据此通过最大EP阶数确定等效噪声源数目并预测高阶统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见测量的功率谱不足以刻画真实环境的非高斯涨落，无法区分低阶统计量相同但高阶统计不同的环境，因此需要新的噪声表征手段。

Method: 分析噪声平均退相干响应中的异常点；提出在保持功率谱不变的前提下，用多个相同且独立的噪声源描述退相干环境；由校准的单源模型出发，从实测退相干响应中提取最大EP阶数，并以原子干涉（atomtronic）读出将不同EP特征映射到系综平均原子干涉图样的演化上。

Result: EP阶数可区分低阶统计相同、仅高阶统计不同的噪声环境；最大EP阶数对应噪声源数目，并能预测合成噪声的高阶统计量。

Conclusion: 将非厄米动力学与环境噪声统计相联系，为量子计算、计量与精密测量中量子器件的噪声表征提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Efficient control of quantum devices requires the knowledge of their environmental noise, yet the commonly measured power spectrum does not fully characterize non-Gaussian fluctuations of realistic environments. Here we show that exceptional points (EPs) in the noise-averaged dephasing response can expose observable signatures of non-Gaussian statistics, distinguishing environments with identical lower-order statistics. We also introduce a theoretical model that describes dephasing environments in terms of identical independent noise sources while preserving the power spectrum. Given a calibrated single-source model, the largest EP order extracted from the measured dephasing response determines the number of sources and predicts the higher-order statistics of the combined noise. We illustrate an atomtronic readout that maps distinct EP signatures onto an evolving ensemble-averaged atomic interference pattern. Our results connect non-Hermitian dynamics to environmental noise statistics, providing a route to characterize noise in quantum devices used in computing, metrology, and precision measurement.

</details>


### [113] [Goldbach Representations in Quantum Optics and Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38675)
*Akira Sone,Maxim Olshanii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文在量子光学框架下实现了哥德巴赫表示：偶数Fock态经随机分束器并被投影到素数子空间，成功事件即为哥德巴赫表示；结合Hardy–Littlewood渐近公式，证明固定透射率后测量态在相位估计中呈标准量子极限标度，而哥德巴赫表示的等权叠加呈Heisenberg标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哥德巴赫猜想及哥德巴赫表示计数属于数论问题，而量子测量统计与量子度量学提供了新的交叉视角。作者希望把哥德巴赫表示的计数统计映射到可实现的量子光学过程，并探讨这种数论统计结构是否对应不同的量子精密测量标度。

Method: 将偶数N的Fock态输入透射率服从均匀分布的随机分束器，对两输出模式做素数子空间投影；证明每次成功探测对应一个哥德巴赫表示，且平均探测概率正比于哥德巴赫表示数目。随后借助Hardy–Littlewood渐近公式，比较固定透射率分束器产生的后测量态与哥德巴赫表示等权叠加态在相位估计中的最终精度极限（量子Fisher信息标度）。

Result: 平均探测概率严格正比于哥德巴赫表示数，给出哥德巴赫表示的量子光学实现；固定透射率情形下后测量态仅呈标准量子极限（∝1/√N 型）标度，而哥德巴赫表示的等权叠加呈Heisenberg极限（∝1/N 型）标度。

Conclusion: 哥德巴赫表示的计数统计可在量子光学装置中直接实现，并与量子度量学中的精度标度相联系；素数结构诱导的叠加态可提供超越标准量子极限的Heisenberg级相位估计能力，为数论统计与量子信息/量子计量之间搭建了桥梁。

Abstract: We derive a relation between Goldbach representations and photon-number statistics in a quantum-optical setup. Considering an even-number Fock state injected into a stochastic beam splitter with uniformly distributed transmissivity and projections of the two output modes onto prime subspaces, we demonstrate that each successful outcome corresponds to an Goldbach representation and that the averaged detection probability is exactly proportional to the number of Goldbach representations. Then, employing the Hardy--Littlewood asymptotic formula, we demonstrate that the post-measurement state generated by a beam splitter with fixed transmissivity exhibits standard quantum limit scaling of the ultimate precision limit in terms of phase estimation, whereas an equally weighted superposition of Goldbach representations exhibits Heisenberg scaling. These results provide a quantum-optical realization of Goldbach representations and relate their statistics of with quantum metrology.

</details>


### [114] [Sample-Based Quantum Diagonalization for Variable Molecular Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38701)
*Matthieu Fortin-Deschenes,Ibrahim Shehzad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出变几何采样量子对角化（VGSQD）算法，将SQD从单一固定几何推广到变几何，通过复用参考几何的量子样本显著降低量子计算成本，并在124量子比特处理器上完成几何优化与反应路径计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子空间方法（如SQD）已能处理经典困难体系，但实用算法仍多局限于电子基态与激发态能量计算，缺乏面向几何优化与反应路径等实际化学任务的高效量子算法。

Method: 将采样量子对角化（SQD）扩展到可变分子几何：在参考几何处获得的量子采样被复用于位移几何，从而大幅减少量子线路采样开销；使用最多124量子比特的线路在IBM Heron r2处理器ibm-quebec上执行。

Result: 确定了弱束缚强关联的铍二聚体与水分子分子平衡几何和能量；并将VGSQD扩展至化学反应，计算氢交换反应的最小能量路径与能量势垒；结果与CCSD(T)、DMRG等先进经典方法相当。

Conclusion: VGSQD将量子化学算法的适用范围从能量计算拓展至几何优化与反应路径，二者对实际应用至关重要，为实用化量子化学计算提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum and hybrid quantum-classical algorithms have the potential to improve the accuracy and scale of quantum chemistry and impact a broad range of applications such as drug discovery and catalysis. In particular, subspace methods such as sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD) can already tackle classically challenging systems. However, practical algorithms that go beyond the computation of electronic ground and excited states are still scarce. Here, we introduce the variable-geometry sample-based quantum diagonalization (VGSQD) algorithm, which extends SQD to variable molecular geometries by reusing quantum samples from a reference geometry at displaced ones, thus significantly reducing quantum computational cost. Using circuits of up to 124 qubits on ibm-quebec, an IBM Heron r2 quantum processor, we determine the equilibrium geometry and energy of the weakly bound, strongly correlated beryllium dimer, as well as the water molecule. Finally, we extend VGSQD to chemical reactions and compute the minimum energy path and energy barrier of a hydrogen exchange reaction. The results are comparable with state-of-the-art classical methods such as CCSD(T) and DMRG. This work broadens the scope of quantum algorithms for chemistry, from energies to geometry optimization and reaction pathways, both essential for practical applications.

</details>


### [115] [Quenched Thermodynamics from Ancilla-Assisted Imaginary-Time Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38707)
*Ibukunoluwa Adisa,Daniele Cuomo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种ancilla辅助虚时演化制备近似Gibbs态的量子算法，利用测量记录识别规范类，从而计算横场Ising模型的淬火无序平均；在3×3开边界格子上验证，可分辨全部16个磁通类并估计淬火能量与自由能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬火热力学需在固定耦合构型下求平衡物理并对无序分布求平均，一般难以近似求解；现有方法难以在处理阻挫自旋系统的同时按指定无序分布（独立于电路采样频率）进行加权。

Method: 将耦合构型组织为规范类；用ancilla辅助的虚时演化制备近似Gibbs态，每次测量时由ancilla记录识别能量估计所属的规范类，从而按预设无序分布加权求淬火平均；并通过热力学积分将重建扩展到淬火自由能。

Result: 在开边界3×3格子上验证：单步电路可分辨全部16个磁通类；有限采样估计了独立分布键符号下的淬火能量；热力学积分给出淬火自由能估计；与精确对角化比较表明有限步近似是主要误差来源。

Conclusion: 测量记录可解耦无序分布与电路采样频率，使量子算法能够访问阻挫自旋系统的热力学性质。

Abstract: Quenched thermodynamics combines the equilibrium physics of fixed coupling configurations with a prescribed distribution of disorder. Problems related to quenched thermodynamics are generally intractable to approximate. We provide a quantum algorithm to calculate quenched disorder averages for the transverse-field Ising model, whose coupling configurations are organized into gauge classes. We show how to estimate these averages by preparing approximate Gibbs states through ancilla-assisted imaginary-time evolution. At each measurement, the ancilla record identifies the gauge class to which the energy estimate contributes.
  We validate our framework through an open $3\times3$ lattice. A one-step circuit resolves all sixteen flux classes, and a finite-shot calculation estimates the quenched energy for independently distributed bond signs. Thermodynamic integration extends the reconstruction to a quenched free-energy estimate, while comparison with exact diagonalization identifies the finite-step approximation as the dominant source of error. This use of the measurement record provides access to the thermodynamics of frustrated spin systems with a disorder distribution chosen independently of the circuit's sampling frequencies.

</details>


### [116] [Generalized Mean-Field Ising Model as a Benchmark Family with Exact Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38803)
*Madita Willsch,Gopal Chandra Santra,Dennis Willsch,Samuel Morales,Lukas Mauth,Vrinda Mehta,Fengping Jin,Kristel Michielsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出广义平均场Ising（GMFI）模型族——稠密连接、非均匀但基态可精确求解的Ising哈密顿量，并揭示其隐藏低维结构，可用于在远超精确对角化的规模上基准测试量子与经典优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既能解析求解又具有挑战性优化结构的非平凡模型极为稀缺；现有精确可解模型难以作为可扩展的算法基准。

Method: 构造GMFI哈密顿量族并严格求解基态，给出系统尺寸与简并基态的数论分类；结合量子退火理论分析、D-Wave量子处理器实验、QAOA、虚时演化（ITE）、贪心算法、模拟退火及量子启发式贪心算法进行系统比较。

Result: 尽管模型精确可解，其算法行为非平凡：量子退火能隙虽预期随系统规模增大而减小，却呈现显著的、依赖系统规模的振荡特征；各方法在不同规模下表现出可区分的性能差异。

Conclusion: GMFI模型在精确多体理论、数论、优化与量子硬件之间建立了罕见桥梁，为识别和理解经典与量子计算的算法局限性提供了可扩展基准。

Abstract: Exactly solvable models provide opportunities to understand complex optimization problems while offering controlled benchmarks for computational methods. However, nontrivial models that combine analytical tractability with challenging optimization structure remain scarce. Here, we introduce the Generalized Mean-Field Ising (GMFI) family, a densely connected and inhomogeneous class of Ising Hamiltonians whose ground states can nevertheless be obtained exactly. This exact solution reveals a hidden low-dimensional structure and enables a complete number-theoretic classification of different system sizes and degenerate ground states. Uniquely, the GMFI family can be used to benchmark both quantum and classical optimization methods over system sizes far beyond exact diagonalization. We investigate the GMFI family using several approaches, including theoretical analysis of quantum annealing, experiments on a D-Wave quantum processor, the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA), imaginary-time evolution (ITE), greedy algorithm, simulated annealing, and a quantum-inspired greedy algorithm. Despite the GMFI model's exact solvability, it exhibits nontrivial algorithmic behavior: although the quantum-annealing gap is expected to decrease with increasing system size, it shows pronounced and unexpected system-size-dependent oscillatory signatures. These results establish GMFI models as a rare bridge between exact many-body theory, number theory, optimization, and quantum hardware, providing a scalable benchmark for identifying and understanding algorithmic limitations in classical and quantum computing.

</details>


### [117] [Generating Multipartite Entanglement via Structured-Bath Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38807)
*Baptiste Debecker,Philipp Westhoff,Visal So,Sebastian Paeckel,Guido Pagano,John Martin,Mattia Moroder,François Damanet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用阿贝尔强对称性与时间浴关联，在相互独立的局域浴中耗散性地制备真正的多体纠缠稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在无相互作用于系统间的独立局域库条件下，仅靠耗散与对称性制备真正多体纠缠稳态的机制。

Method: 在相互作用Ising链的弱耦合理论框架下，借助阿贝尔强对称性将非二次型生成元的稳态构造约化为经典问题；并用结构化浴将时间浴关联转化为经典空间关联，再经强对称性提升为量子关联；辅以精确马尔可夫嵌入与张量网络仿真，以及基于协同冷却运动模式的囚禁离子实现方案。

Result: 平坦浴会指数抹除Ising轴关联；结构化浴可将时间关联转为空间关联并经强对称性产生全局多体纠缠；强对称性使任意Ising耦合下都能精确求得稳态，并揭示经典可区分性与纠缠的精确联系；通过工程化浴谱可增大关联长度，从可分初态制备类GHZ态。

Conclusion: 该机制适用于超导与囚禁离子平台，并给出了囚禁离子协同冷却运动模式的具体实现方案。

Abstract: We present how to dissipatively prepare genuinely multipartite-entangled steady states with mutually independent local reservoirs by harnessing an Abelian strong symmetry and temporal bath correlations. In interacting Ising chains, we demonstrate that, for a broad class of system-environment couplings, flat baths exponentially erase Ising-axis correlations. Structured baths overcome this obstruction by converting temporal bath correlations into classical spatial correlations. Strong symmetry then promotes these correlations to the quantum domain, enabling global multipartite entanglement. Within weak-coupling theory, we obtain exact steady states despite the generator's nonquadratic nature: strong symmetry reduces their construction to a classical problem for arbitrary Ising couplings. This reduction reveals an exact connection between classical distinguishability and entanglement. We then show how to engineer the bath spectrum to increase the correlation length and enable the preparation of GHZ-like states from separable initial conditions. We test the mechanism beyond the analytical regime using exact Markovian embeddings and tensor-network simulations. Our approach is particularly suited to superconducting and trapped-ion platforms, and we propose a concrete implementation for the latter using sympathetically cooled motional modes.

</details>


### [118] [Key Evaluation of a Relativistic Quantum Key Distribution Over a Turbulent Free-space Optical Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38808)
*G. Bebrov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究湍流自由空间信道上单光子和相干态相对论量子密钥分发（RQKD）的性能，建立将湍流相位涨落纳入密钥率分析的理论模型并做数值仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由空间RQKD在湍流大气中运行，湍流对干涉可见度与成码的影响缺乏定量刻画，需要评估可达通信距离与密钥率并给出设计指南。

Method: 构建理论模型，将湍流引起的相位涨落引入干涉可见度与密钥率分析；在真实信道条件下进行数值仿真，考察干涉臂间距r（或信号与参考态时间间隔ΔT）与Fried参数之比对性能的影响。

Result: 湍流通过降低干涉可见度使协议性能下降，退化程度由r（或ΔT）与Fried参数之比决定；单光子协议在所有考察的湍流区间均可工作，而相干态实现强烈依赖干涉仪几何（尤其ΔT），存在安全成码失效的阈值。

Conclusion: 定量刻画了大气湍流对RQKD的影响，为自由空间RQKD系统的设计与优化提供实用准则。

Abstract: We investigate the performance of relativistic quantum key distribution (RQKD) over turbulent free-space optical channels for both single-photon and coherent-state implementations. A theoretical model is developed to describe the effect of atmospheric turbulence on interferometric visibility and secret-key generation by incorporating turbulence-induced phase fluctuations into the key-rate analysis. Numerical simulations are performed under realistic channel conditions to evaluate the achievable communication distance and secret-key rate. The results show that atmospheric turbulence degrades the protocol performance by reducing interference visibility, with the severity of the degradation determined by the ratio between the interferometer-arm separation $r$ (or temporal separation of signal and reference states $ΔT$) and Fried's parameter. The single-photon protocol remains operational over all investigated turbulence regimes, whereas the coherent-state implementation exhibits a critical dependence on the interferometer geometry (especially on $ΔT$), leading to a threshold beyond which secure key generation is no longer possible. These findings quantify the impact of atmospheric turbulence on relativistic quantum key distribution and provide practical guidelines for the design and optimization of free-space RQKD systems.

</details>


### [119] [Memory-Induced Limits on Entanglement Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38817)
*Roberto Benjamin Salazar Vargas,Hayden Zammit,Trevor Spiteri,Gianluca Valentino,Tony J. G. Apollaro,Johann A. Briffa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究不经复位即重复使用的量子信道的纠缠分发极限，给出量子容量的解析下界、与Holevo-Werner SDP上界结合，并给出纠缠破坏判据与定时误差阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中量子信道会被反复使用而不重新初始化，前次使用遗留的残余激发构成记忆源，会逐渐压低相干量子传输能力，但已有容量界未刻画复用次数与链长共同作用下的这一限制。

Method: 基于有效重复使用信道描述建模残余激发积累；推导量子容量的解析下界，并与Holevo-Werner半正定规划上界结合形成容量窗口；提出识别纠缠破坏信道的解析判据，阈值同时依赖链长与复用次数。

Result: 对完美态转移链，所得容量窗口比以往界更紧，且越接近纠缠破坏阈值相对改进越显著；第二次使用时下界证明所有含内记忆链的最大相对定时误差容忍度略低于22.63%，由三格点链达到；并给出36.41%的基本相对定时误差阈值，超过该值即可解析保证任何含内记忆链的零无辅助量子容量，且与使用次数无关。

Conclusion: 结果为重复纠缠分发建立了清晰的基准模型，并将定时精度、链长和记忆积累确认为可扩展量子网络架构的基本约束。

Abstract: We investigate the operational limits of entanglement distribution through quantum channels reused without reinitialization. Building on an effective repeated-use channel description, we show that residual excitations left in the channel from previous uses act as a memory source that progressively suppresses coherent quantum transmission. To quantify this limitation, we derive an analytical lower bound on the quantum capacity and combine it with the Holevo-Werner semidefinite-programming upper bound. We also provide an analytical criterion to identify entanglement-breaking channels, with thresholds that depend jointly on chain length and reuse. For perfect-state-transfer chains, the resulting capacity window yields tighter estimates than previous bounds, with the relative improvement becoming substantially more pronounced closer to the entanglement-breaking threshold. Moreover, on the second use, our lower bound certifies a maximal relative timing-error tolerance across all chains with internal memory that lies just below 22.63% and is attained by the three-site chain. We further derive a fundamental relative timing-error threshold of 36.41%, above which we analytically guarantee zero unassisted quantum capacity for every chain with internal memory, independent of the number of uses. These results establish a clear benchmark model for repeated entanglement distribution, and identify timing precision, chain length, and memory accumulation as fundamental constraints on scalable quantum-network architectures.

</details>


### [120] [Gravitational Interactions in Nuclear Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38832)
*Konstantin Beyer,Niklas E. Önne,Shimon Kolkowitz,Dolev Bluvstein,Mikhail Lukin,Jun Ye,Igor Pikovski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究提出利用大系综核钟（如固态钍钟）的核跃迁观测原子间相互引力作用，评估其引力红移与量子引力耦合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子钟精度与原子数持续提升，核钟将钟态编码于核跃迁而非电子跃迁，为精密测量开辟新前沿；本文探究大系综核钟能否用于观测核间相互引力作用。

Method: 理论分析邻近原子引起的引力红移，包括以核能叠加态作为引力源所产生的真正量子引力相互作用；通过空间对称性与高精度光谱学提出抑制电磁场效应的方案；讨论用外部引力场传感器直接探测能量诱导的质量叠加；并分析自发辐射导致的退相干对引力诱导纠缠的影响。

Result: 对于宏观数量的核（如固态钍钟），互引力引起的钟频移可能不可忽略；能量诱导的质量叠加可通过外部引力场传感器直接探测；而观测涉及钟的引力诱导纠缠因自发辐射退相干，需要大得多的实验进步。

Conclusion: 核钟单独或与光机械系统等互补传感器结合，可能成为在短距离探测引力、检验超越牛顿极限的引力量子源质量的未来途径。

Abstract: Atomic clocks achieve extraordinary precision through quantum control over large ensembles of atoms, with precision increasing and atom number advancing steadily. Emerging nuclear clock technology encodes clock states in nuclear rather than electronic transitions, offering a new frontier for precision measurement. Here we study whether a large ensemble of such clock nuclei could be used to observe their mutual gravitational interaction. We investigate the gravitational redshift induced by neighboring atoms, including nuclear energy superpositions as sources of gravity, giving rise to a genuinely quantum gravitational interaction between pairs of nuclei. We find that the resulting clock frequency shift due to mutual gravity may become non-negligible for a macroscopic number of nuclei, for instance in a solid-state thorium clock, and we propose approaches to mitigate electromagnetic field effects by exploiting spatial symmetry and high-precision spectroscopy. We further discuss how energy-induced mass superpositions could be directly probed using external gravitational field sensors. By contrast, observing gravity-induced entanglement involving clocks would require substantially greater experimental advances due to decoherence from spontaneous emission.
